*Antoine Legrand,Renaud Detry,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展神经辐射场（NeRF）的方法，通过引入每张图像的可学习外观嵌入和姿态校正项，解决了在轨非合作航天器3D重建中光照变化大和位姿信息不准确的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨自主交会与接近操作中，需要对非合作未知航天器进行3D模型离线重建，但面临两个主要挑战：在轨光照条件变化大且快速，以及图像位姿信息不准确导致重建不确定性。

Method: 在NeRF基础上为每张图像引入两个可学习参数：一个外观嵌入（appearance embedding）用于捕获特定图像的光照条件，一个姿态校正项（pose correction term）用于精化有噪声的位姿标签，两者与NeRF联合学习，增加极小复杂度。

Result: 在三组代表性在轨操作图像集上验证，该方法显著提升了光照变化和位姿误差下的鲁棒性，在离线重建中表现有效，并展示了其适用于在线重建的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的每图像参数扩展，NeRF能够有效应对在轨非合作航天器重建中的光照和位姿两大难题，为主动碎片清除和在轨服务提供了可行的3D重建方案。

Abstract: Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations around uncooperative, unknown spacecraft are critical for active debris removal and on-orbit servicing missions. A key component of such operations is the offline reconstruction of a 3D model of the target from a set of 2D images. This task is challenging due to two main factors. First, in-orbit illumination conditions exhibit considerable variability, and change rapidly over time. Second, the inaccuracy of pose information in the images, results in 3D reconstruction uncertainty. To overcome these challenges, we propose to extend Neural Radiance Fields with per-image degrees of freedom: a learnable appearance embedding that captures the illumination conditions specific to each image, and an image-specific pose correction term that refines its noisy pose label to increase 3D consistency across images. These parameters add minimal complexity, as they are learned jointly with the NeRF, yet they substantially improve robustness to illumination variability and pose inaccuracies. We validate our approach on three image sets representative of in-orbit operations, demonstrating its effectiveness for offline reconstruction and highlighting its suitability for online reconstruction, an open problem in the field.

</details>


### [222] [Code-as-Room: Generating 3D Rooms from Top-Down View Images via Agentic Code Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18451)
*Yixuan Yang,Zhen Luo,Wanshui Gan,Jinkun Hao,Junru Lu,Jinghao Yan,Zhaoyang Lyu,Xudong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Code-as-Room框架，通过MLLM智能体将俯视房间图解析为Blender代码，实现结构化3D室内场景生成，并引入跨阶段记忆模块和专用基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM方法中，基于文本难以捕获精确空间信息，基于图像的条件智能体在生成完整俯视房间时存在不稳定和无限循环问题。

Method: 提出Code-as-Room框架：①MLLM智能体解析俯视房间图，提取场景元素与空间关系；②采用多阶段管道生成可执行的Blender代码（几何、材质、光照）；③引入跨阶段记忆模块缓解上下文遗忘问题。

Result: 在自建基准上，与现有智能体方法进行对比，验证了所提执行框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Code-as-Room通过代码驱动的结构化生成方式，有效解决了图像条件智能体在3D室内场景合成中的不稳定性和记忆遗忘问题。

Abstract: Designing realistic and functional 3D indoor rooms is essential for a wide range of applications, including interior design, virtual reality, gaming, and embodied AI. While recent MLLM-based approaches have shown great potential for 3D room synthesis from textual descriptions or reference images, text-based methods struggle to capture precise spatial information, and existing image-conditioned agents suffer from instability and infinite looping when tasked with holistic room generation from top-down views. To address these limitations, we propose Code-as-Room, an MLLM-based agentic framework equipped with a structured execution harness, which represents 3D rooms with Blender codes. Given a top-down room image, the framework parses the reference image to extract scene elements and their spatial relationships, and synthesizes executable Blender code for geometry, materials, and lighting in a principled, multi-stage pipeline. A cross-stage memory module is maintained throughout to mitigate context forgetting inherent to existing agent-based frameworks. We further introduce a dedicated benchmark for code-based 3D room synthesis, encompassing various evaluation protocols. Based on our benchmark, comprehensive comparisons against existing agent-based methods are conducted to validate the effectiveness of our proposed execution harness.

</details>


### [223] [PERL: Parameter Efficient Reasoning in CLIP Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18464)
*Simone Carnemolla,Salvatore Calcagno,Daniela Giordano,Concetto Spampinato,Matteo Pennisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PERL框架，通过在冻结的CLIP模型中引入轻量级循环推理模块，在少量参数（约6K）下实现了高效的视觉-语言模型适应，相比参数扩展方法取得了更优的性能-参数权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法主要通过增加可训练参数来提升任务专业化能力，作者探索另一种互补思路：能否通过潜在表示的迭代推理来实现适应，而非单纯依赖参数数量增加。

Method: PERL在冻结的CLIP模型中添加一个轻量级共享推理模块，在多个细化步骤中循环应用。每步生成一个潜在推理token，注入到中间编码层，逐步优化高层语义表示，同时保留CLIP预训练的多模态结构。

Result: 在15个基准测试中（涵盖基础到新颖类泛化、跨数据集迁移、分布外ImageNet变体），PERL在快速适应的少样本设置下取得最佳性能-参数权衡，新颖类准确率高，仅约6K可训练参数，比最大对比方法少817倍。

Conclusion: 迭代潜在推理为判别式视觉-语言模型提供了一种不同于参数扩展的互补适应机制。

Abstract: Contrastively trained vision-language models such as CLIP provide strong zero-shot transfer by aligning images and text in a shared embedding space. However, adapting these models to downstream tasks without degrading their open-vocabulary generalization remains challenging. Existing parameter-efficient adaptation methods typically improve task specialization through learned prompts, adapters, or multimodal transformations, where adaptation capacity is primarily expressed through additional trainable parameters. Inspired by recent latent reasoning methods in language models, we investigate a complementary perspective: can adaptation emerge from iterative reasoning on latent representations rather than from increasing parameter count alone? We introduce PERL (Parameter-Efficient Reasoning in CLIP Latent Space), a lightweight adaptation framework that augments a frozen CLIP model with a compact shared reasoning module applied recurrently across refinement steps. At each step, PERL generates a latent reasoning token conditioned on the current representation and injects it into an intermediate encoder layer, progressively refining higher-level semantic representations while preserving CLIP's pretrained multimodal structure. Across 15 benchmarks spanning base-to-novel generalization, cross-dataset transfer, and out-of-distribution ImageNet variants, PERL achieves the best parameter-performance trade-off among the compared methods under a fast-adaptation few-shot setting, combining strong novel-class accuracy and competitive transfer performance with only about 6K trainable parameters, up to 817x fewer than the largest compared approach. Overall, our results suggest that iterative latent reasoning provides a complementary adaptation mechanism to parameter scaling in discriminative vision-language models.

</details>


### [224] [Speech-Guided Multimodal Learning for Vocal Tract Segmentation in Real-Time MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18466)
*Daiqi Liu,Lukas Mulzer,Md Hasan,Nyvenn de Castro,Fangxu Xing,Xingjian Kang,Chengze Ye,Siyuan Mei,Yipeng Sun,Tomás Arias-Vergara,Jana Hutter,Jonghye Woo,Andreas Maier,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段框架，在训练时利用声学和音韵监督，推理时仅需实时MRI图像，通过跨模态对比预训练和交叉注意力解码器将多模态知识迁移到单模态推理中，在两项数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声道的实时MRI分割方法忽略了同步采集的声学信号，少数多模态方法在音频不可用时无法部署，亟需一种既能利用多模态优势又能在单模态下推理的方法。

Method: 三阶段框架：1）音韵表示转换为空间边界框先验用于发音器官定位；2）通过双层级跨模态对比预训练对齐视觉和声学编码器；3）通过交叉注意力解码器融合学习到的表征，将多模态知识迁移到单模态推理流程。

Result: 在75-Speaker~Annot-16和USC-TIMIT数据集上，该方法在分割精度上优于现有单模态和多模态方法。

Conclusion: 多模态监督可以为精确且临床可部署的声道分割提供可迁移的益处，训练阶段融合音频信息能有效提升纯图像推理的性能。

Abstract: Segmenting vocal tract articulators in real-time MRI (rtMRI) is a challenging dynamic image segmentation problem characterized by low contrast, rapid motion, and limited spatial resolution. However, while rtMRI acquisitions may provide synchronized acoustic signals, existing methods discard this information, and the few multimodal approaches that incorporate audio cannot be deployed when audio is unavailable. We propose a three-stage framework that leverages acoustic and phonological supervision during training while requiring only the rtMRI image at inference: phonological representations are converted into spatial bounding-box priors for articulator localization, visual and acoustic encoders are aligned via dual-level cross-modal contrastive pretraining, and the learned representations are fused through a cross-attention decoder, effectively transferring multimodal knowledge into a single-modality inference pipeline. Evaluated on 75-Speaker~Annot-16 and USC-TIMIT datasets, our method outperforms existing unimodal and multimodal methods, demonstrating that multimodal supervision provides transferable benefits for precise and clinically deployable vocal tract segmentation.

</details>


### [225] [Weakly Supervised Cross-Modal Learning for 4D Radar Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18507)
*Jingyun Fu,Zhiyu Xiang,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅利用图像和里程计进行弱监督的4D雷达场景流估计框架(IterFlow)，通过2D跟踪和分割提供实例级语义指导，在VoD数据集上超越现有跨模态监督和全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达场景流估计缺少真实标注数据，现有自监督方法因雷达低保真度而效果不佳，跨模态监督方法需要昂贵的LiDAR传感器和复杂的多任务架构。

Method: 提出弱监督迭代框架，利用现成的2D跟踪和分割算法获取跟踪实例掩码，反投影到3D空间提供实例级语义指导；结合车辆里程计和雷达运动线索构建刚性静态损失。

Result: 在View-of-Delft(VoD)真实数据集上，不仅超越依赖3D多目标跟踪的跨模态监督方法，也优于现有全监督场景流估计方法。

Conclusion: 仅使用图像和里程计进行弱监督即可实现高效雷达场景流估计，避免了LiDAR依赖，代码已开源。

Abstract: Due to the difficulty of obtaining ground-truth data for 4D radar scene flow estimation, previous methods typically rely on either self-supervised losses or cross-modal supervision using 3D LiDAR data, 2D images, and odometry. However, self-supervised approaches often yield suboptimal results due to radar's inherently low-fidelity measurements, while existing cross-modal supervised methods introduce complex multi-task architecture and require costly LiDAR sensors to generate pseudo radar scene flow labels from pretrained 3D tracking models. To overcome these limitations, we propose a task-specific iterative framework for weakly supervised radar scene flow learning, using only images and odometry for auxiliary supervision during training. Specially, we establish two novel instance-aware self-supervised losses by exploiting off-the-shelf 2D tracking and segmentation algorithms to obtain tracked instance masks, which are back-projected into 3D space to provide instance-level semantic guidance; for static regions, we integrate vehicle odometry with radar's intrinsic motion cues to construct a rigid static loss. Extensive experiments on the real-world View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset demonstrate that our method not only surpasses state-of-the-art cross-modal supervised approaches that rely on 3D multi-object tracking on dense LiDAR point clouds but also outperforms existing fully supervised scene flow estimation methods. The code is open-sourced at \href{https://github.com/FuJingyun/IterFlow}{https://github.com/FuJingyun/IterFlow}.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond Morphology: Quantifying the Diagnostic Power of Color Features in Cancer Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18522)
*Farnaz Kheiri,Shahryar Rahnamayan,Masoud Makrehchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了仅凭颜色特征（排除形态学信息）在癌症分类中的判别能力，发现简单的全局颜色特征在良恶性分类任务中准确率可达89%，表明颜色分布本身包含非随机的诊断信号，可作为高效的预筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类病理学家主要利用颜色增强对比度来解读组织形态，而机器视觉模型将颜色作为原始统计信息处理。这种差异引出一个基本问题：像素强度（颜色）本身在缺乏结构和形态线索的情况下，能在多大程度上支持癌症分类？

Method: 提取统计颜色矩以及离散化的RGB和HSV颜色直方图，在10种不同的实验设置中，使用经典机器学习分类器评估这些全局颜色特征在排除所有形态信息后的分类性能。

Result: 仅凭颜色特征在良恶性二分类诊断任务中准确率高达89%，且始终显著优于随机基线，表明原始颜色分布编码了非随机的、与诊断相关的癌症信号。

Conclusion: 简单且计算高效的颜色特征可作为有效的预筛查工具。通过识别具有强烈恶性颜色指标的样本，这些轻量级模型可以充当第一道分诊系统，减少对复杂深度学习架构的计算负担。

Abstract: In histopathology, human experts primarily rely on color as a means of enhancing contrast to interpret tissue morphology, whereas machine vision models process color as raw statistical information. This distinction raises a fundamental question: to what extent can pixel intensity alone, independent of structural and morphological cues, support cancer classification? To address this question, we systematically evaluated the standalone discriminative power of global color features while deliberately excluding all morphological information. Specifically, we extracted statistical color moments and discretized RGB and HSV color histograms, and assessed their performance across ten diverse experimental settings using classical machine learning classifiers. Our results demonstrate that color features alone can achieve strong performance in binary diagnostic tasks (e.g., benign versus malignant), with classification accuracies reaching up to 89%. This performance is likely attributable to global chromatic shifts associated with malignancy. Importantly, these simple color-based representations consistently outperformed random baselines by a substantial margin, indicating that raw color distributions encode a non-random and diagnostically relevant signal for cancer detection. Consequently, this study suggests that simple, computationally efficient color features can serve as an effective pre-screening tool. By identifying samples with strong chromatic indicators of malignancy, these lightweight models could function as a first-pass triage system, reducing the computational burden on complex deep learning architectures.

</details>


### [227] [LESSViT: Robust Hyperspectral Representation Learning under Spectral Configuration Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18541)
*Haozhe Si,Yuxuan Wan,Yuqing Wang,Minh Do,Han Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LESSViT提出了一种基于低秩分解的高效空间-光谱ViT架构，通过LESS Attention将全空间-光谱注意力的复杂度从O(N²C²)降至O(rNC)，并结合通道无关的patch嵌入和波长感知位置编码，以及HyperMAE预训练策略，实现了跨传感器的高光谱图像泛化。在SpectralEarth基准上，该方法在保持分布内竞争力的同时，显著提升了对光谱偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同传感器的高光谱图像在波长覆盖、波段采样和通道维度上存在差异，导致固定光谱配置下训练的模型难以泛化到其他传感器。现有ViT方法要么依赖隐式光谱建模（固定通道假设），要么采用显式空间-光谱注意力但计算成本过高，存在效率与表达能力之间的根本性权衡。

Method: 提出LESSViT架构，核心是LESS Attention——通过可分离的空间和光谱分量进行结构化低秩分解，将全空间-光谱注意力复杂度从O(N²C²)降至O(rNC)。还包括通道无关的patch嵌入和波长感知位置编码以支持灵活光谱输入，以及HyperMAE预训练策略（解耦空间-光谱掩码+层次化通道采样）。

Result: 在SpectralEarth基准的跨光谱泛化设置下，LESSViT显著提升了对光谱偏移的鲁棒性，同时在分布内任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式且高效的空间-光谱建模对于可扩展和可泛化的高光谱表示学习至关重要，LESSViT有效解决了跨传感器泛化中的效率与表达能力权衡问题。

Abstract: Modeling hyperspectral imagery (HSI) across different sensors presents a fundamental challenge due to variations in wavelength coverage, band sampling, and channel dimensionality. As a result, models trained under a fixed spectral configuration often fail to generalize to other sensors. Existing Vision Transformer (ViT) approaches either rely on implicit spectral modeling with fixed channel assumptions or adopt explicit spatial-spectral attention with prohibitive computational cost, leading to a fundamental trade-off between efficiency and expressiveness. In this work, we introduce Low-rank Efficient Spatial-Spectral ViT (LESSViT), a sensor-flexible architecture for cross-spectral generalization. LESSViT is built on LESS Attention, a structured low-rank factorization that models joint spatial-spectral interactions through separable spatial and spectral components, reducing the complexity of full spatial-spectral attention from $O(N^2 C^2)$ to $O(rNC)$, where $N$ is the number of spatial tokens, $C$ is the number of spectral channels, and $r$ is the rank of the low-rank approximation. We further incorporate channel-agnostic patch embedding and wavelength-aware positional encoding to support flexible spectral inputs. To enable efficient and robust pretraining, we introduce a hyperspectral masked autoencoder (HyperMAE) with decoupled spatial-spectral masking and hierarchical channel sampling. We evaluate LESSViT under a cross-spectral generalization setting that simulates cross-sensor variability. Experiments on the SpectralEarth benchmark demonstrate that LESSViT improves robustness under spectral shifts while remaining competitive in-distribution, and explicit and efficient spatial-spectral modeling is essential for scalable and generalizable hyperspectral representation learning.

</details>


### [228] [OmniPro: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Omni-Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18577)
*Ruixiang Zhao,Jie Yang,Zijie Xin,Tianyi Wang,Fengyun Rao,Jing LYU,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniPro是首个全面评估全模态主动流式视频理解的基准，包含2700个人工验证样本、9个子任务和双模式评估协议，发现音频能提升性能但利用率差异大、模型长期鲁棒性不足、非语音音频感知最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在三个方面存在不足：过度依赖视觉信号、采用轮询或固定时间戳而非真正的主动评估、覆盖任务范围有限，无法可靠评估和区分全模态主动流式模型。

Method: 构建包含2700个人工验证样本的基准，覆盖9个子任务和3个认知层次、6种基本视频理解能力；其中84%样本需要音频信号，每个样本标注模态隔离标签；引入双模式评估协议：Probe模式（在触发前后查询模型）和Online模式（模型自主决定响应时机）。

Result: 评测11个代表性模型发现：（1）音频带来一致性能提升但利用率差异大；（2）性能随时间显著下降，长期鲁棒性有限；（3）非语音音频感知是最薄弱维度。

Conclusion: OmniPro为全模态主动流式视频理解提供了可靠的评估基准，揭示了当前模型在音频利用、长期鲁棒性和非语音音频感知方面的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Omni-proactive streaming video understanding, i.e., autonomously deciding when to speak and what to say from continuous audio-visual streams, is an emerging capability of omni-modal large language models. Existing benchmarks fall short in three key aspects: they rely primarily on visual signals, adopt polling or fixed-timestamp protocols instead of true proactive evaluation, and cover only a limited range of tasks, preventing reliable assessment and differentiation of omni-proactive streaming models. We present OmniPro, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate omni-modal perception, proactive responding, and diverse video understanding tasks. It comprises 2,700 human-verified samples spanning 9 sub-tasks and 3 cognitive levels, covering 6 basic video understanding capabilities. Notably, 84% of samples require audio signals (speech or non-speech), and each sample is annotated with modality-isolation labels to enable fine-grained multimodal analysis. We further introduce a dual-mode evaluation protocol: Probe mode assesses content understanding by querying the model before and after each ground-truth trigger, while Online mode evaluates full proactive ability by requiring models to autonomously decide when to respond in streaming input. Evaluating 11 representative models reveals three key findings: (1) audio provides consistent gains but with highly variable utilization across models, (2) performance degrades significantly over time, indicating limited long-horizon robustness, and (3) non-speech audio perception remains the weakest dimension.

</details>


### [229] [Incantation: Natural Language as the Action Interface for Multi-Entity Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18601)
*Shangwen Zhu,Qianyu Peng,Zhao Pu,Zhilei Shu,Xiangrui Ke,Zhaohu Xing,Zizhao Tong,Zeqing Wang,Xinyu Cui,Huangji Wang,Jian Zhao,Yeying Jin,Fan Cheng,Ruili Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Incantation，首个支持每潜在帧（0.25秒）自然语言条件控制、多实体同时操控、跨实体概念迁移的交互式视频世界模型，基于双向视频骨干、帧级文本交叉注意力、自强制蒸馏和RoPE解耦滑动KV缓存，在跨实体迁移（89% vs 43%）和词汇外提示（90% vs 0%）上超越基线，2步学生模型在480p下达到19.7 FPS并稳定运行2小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型虽然视觉保真度高，但缺乏细粒度的多实体控制能力以及跨实体、跨世界的泛化能力。根本原因在于动作接口设计——标准控制协议（如动画ID、设备输入、场景级描述）在设计时将动作语义绑定到特定实体或引擎上，限制了表达能力和泛化性。

Method: 使用自然语言作为控制接口，结合预训练双向视频骨干与帧级文本交叉注意力；通过ODE初始化的自强制蒸馏（Self-Forcing distillation）与RoPE解耦滑动KV缓存实现实时长时流式生成（2步学生模型480p下19.7 FPS）。

Result: 跨实体转移任务89% vs 43%（Action-Index基线），词汇外提示90% vs 0%；2步学生模型在480p分辨率下达到19.7 FPS，FVD在2小时滚动生成中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 自然语言作为交互界面能够显著提升视频世界模型的多实体控制能力和跨实体泛化能力，且同一架构和训练配方可迁移到不同游戏（如《拳皇》），仅需更换实体动作词汇槽位。

Abstract: Modern interactive video world models have achieved impressive visual fidelity, yet lack fine-grained multi-entity control and cross-entity, cross-world generalization. We trace this gap to the action interface: standard control protocols (e.g. animation IDs, device inputs, scene-level captions) bind action semantics to specific entities or engines at design time. We propose natural language as the interface to unlock expressiveness that no prior interface can achieve, and we present Incantation, the first interactive video world model with per-latent-frame (0.25 s) natural-language conditioning that supports simultaneous multi-entity control and concept-level cross-entity transfer beyond any fixed rendering pipeline. We pair a pretrained bidirectional video backbone with frame-local text cross-attention, and enable real-time long-horizon streaming through ODE-initialized Self-Forcing distillation with a RoPE-decoupled sliding KV-cache. We surpass the Action-Index baseline on cross-entity transfer (89% vs. 43%) and out-of-vocabulary prompts (90% vs. 0%), and our 2-step student sustains 19.7 FPS at 480p with stable FVD over 2-hour rollouts. We further apply the same architecture and training recipe to The King of Fighters, changing only the per-entity action vocabulary slots. We have released a preview subset of the Incantation dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/zhush/incantation-elden-ring-scenes, containing manually collected Elden Ring player-boss combat clips with structured action-oriented metadata. Larger-scale Elden Ring and KOF data will be released with the full project.

</details>


### [230] [Starve to Perceive: Taming Lazy Perception in VLMs with Constrained Visual Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18603)
*Yuhuan Wu,Cong Wei,Fangzhen Lin,Wenhu Chen,Haozhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型在主动感知任务中存在的“懒惰感知”问题（模型模仿感知操作但不依赖其输出），提出“感知饥饿训练”范式，通过限制视觉带宽迫使模型必须进行主动视觉搜索，在无需额外损失函数或架构修改的情况下，在多个基准上取得5%的平均相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLMs的训练范式导致模型学会模仿缩放、裁剪等操作的形式，但并未在功能上依赖这些操作的输出，即“懒惰感知”现象。这是因为粗粒度全局视图结合语言先验已能获得中等精度，模型缺乏学习多步视觉搜索的激励。如果模型不需要主动观察就能成功，它永远不会学会如何观察。

Method: 提出Starve to Perceive训练范式：限制视觉带宽，将每次观察约束在紧凑的token预算内，使得单个视图不足以完成任务，从而迫使主动感知成为唯一可行的策略。该方法无需辅助损失、奖励塑形或架构更改，作为标准后训练管道的即插即用型最小修改。

Result: 在多样化的基准测试上，经过感知饥饿训练的模型取得了5%的平均相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过限制视觉带宽来打破学习不对称性，迫使模型真正依赖主动感知操作，是一种简单有效且无需复杂修改的训练改进方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) deployed as situated agents in high-resolution visual environments require active perception -- the ability to dynamically decide where to look through operations like zooming, cropping, and panning. However, current training paradigms produce models that mimic the surface form of such operations without functionally depending on their outputs, a phenomenon we term lazy perception. We trace this to a fundamental learning asymmetry: when coarse global views combined with language priors suffice for moderate accuracy, the model has no incentive to learn harder multi-step visual search. If a model can succeed without actively looking, it will never learn to look. This motivates Starve to Perceive, a training paradigm that constrains visual bandwidth -- restricting each observation to a tight token budget so that no single view suffices for task completion, making active perception the only viable strategy. Despite requiring no auxiliary losses, reward shaping, or architectural changes -- serving as a minimal, plug-in modification to standard post-training pipelines -- models trained under perceptual starvation achieve substantial gains of 5% average relative improvement across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [231] [CATA: Continual Machine Unlearning via Conflict-Averse Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18610)
*Shen Lin,Junhao Dong,Rongjie Chen,Xiaoyu Zhang,Li Xu,Xiaofeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CATA方法，通过冲突回避的任务向量聚合，解决VLM在持续遗忘场景下遗忘效果、模型保真度和遗忘持久性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM训练数据中涉及隐私、版权和不良内容，需要机器遗忘。现有研究主要关注单次遗忘，但实际部署中常面临连续的遗忘请求，因此需要研究持续遗忘问题。

Method: 提出CATA（冲突回避的任务算术方法），将每次遗忘请求表示为遗忘任务向量，维护历史任务向量，通过符号感知的冲突回避聚合来抑制可能削弱先前遗忘效果的冲突更新分量。

Result: 在单次和持续遗忘设置下，CATA在遗忘效果、模型保真度和遗忘持久性方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CATA有效解决了VLM持续遗忘中的三个关键挑战（遗忘有效性、保真度、持久性），为多模态模型的持续遗忘提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable ability in aligning visual and textual representations, enabling a wide range of multimodal applications. However, their large-scale training data inevitably raises concerns about privacy, copyright, and undesirable content, creating a strong need for machine unlearning. While existing studies mainly focus on single-shot unlearning, practical VLM deployment often involves sequential removal requests over time, giving rise to continual machine unlearning. In this work, we make the first attempt to study continual unlearning for VLMs and identify three key challenges in this setting: effectiveness in removing target knowledge, fidelity in preserving retained model utility, and persistence in preventing knowledge re-emergence under sequential updates. To address these challenges, we propose CATA, a conflict-averse task arithmetic method that represents each forget request as an unlearning task vector. By maintaining historical task vectors and performing sign-aware conflict-averse aggregation, CATA suppresses conflicting update components that may weaken previous forgetting effects. Extensive experiments under both single-shot and continual settings show that CATA outperforms baselines in terms of forgetting effectiveness, model fidelity, and forgetting persistence.

</details>


### [232] [CrossView Suite: Harnessing Cross-view Spatial Intelligence of MLLMs with Dataset, Model and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18621)
*Wei Wang,Yuqian Yuan,Tianwei Lin,Wenqiao Zhang,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossView Suite，通过大规模数据集CrossViewSet（170万样本，17种任务类型）、综合基准CrossViewBench和显式跨视角对齐框架CrossViewer，系统性地提升多模态大语言模型的跨视角空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在跨视角推理方面存在三大瓶颈：缺乏大规模高质量标注训练数据、缺乏系统性评估基准、缺乏显式的跨视角对象级对齐机制。本文旨在填补这些空白，推动MLLMs从单视角感知向真实空间智能演进。

Method: 构建三组件协同的CrossView Suite：1）CrossViewSet：采用多智能体数据引擎生成160万样本、覆盖17种细粒度任务类型的跨视角指令数据集；2）CrossViewBench：场景分离的跨视角空间理解综合基准；3）CrossViewer：遵循感知→对齐→推理的三阶段渐进框架，包含自适应空间区域分词器捕获细粒度对象表征，再显式对齐多视角对象特征，最后融合对齐特征增强跨视角推理能力。

Result: 大量实验和分析表明，大规模训练数据、系统性评估以及显式跨视角对齐三者对于推动MLLMs从单视角感知迈向真实空间智能均至关重要。

Conclusion: CrossView Suite通过数据、基准和方法的系统性方案，有效提升了MLLMs的跨视角空间理解与推理能力，为空间智能的发展提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to move beyond single-view perception and reason consistently about objects, visibility, geometry, and interactions across multiple viewpoints. However, progress in cross-view reasoning remains limited by three major gaps: the scarcity of large-scale well-annotated training data, the lack of comprehensive benchmarks for systematic evaluation, and the absence of explicit alignment mechanisms that establish object-level consistency across views. To address these gaps, we thoroughly develop CrossView Suite across three coordinated components: CrossViewSet, CrossViewBench, and CrossViewer. Firstly, we introduce a multi-agent data engine to meticulously curate a large-scale, high-quality cross-view instruction dataset, termed CrossViewSet, covering 17 fine-grained task types with 1.6M samples. Second, we meticulously create a scene-disjoint CrossViewBench to comprehensively assess the cross-view spatial understanding capability of an MLLM, evaluating it across various aspects. Finally, we propose CrossViewer, a progressive three-stage framework for cross-view spatial reasoning in MLLMs, following a Perception -> Alignment -> Reasoning paradigm. Our method equips an adaptive spatial region tokenizer to capture fine-grained object representations, and then aligns the multi-view objects explicitly, and thus fuses aligned features for boosting the cross-view inference capacity for MLLMs. Extensive experiments and analyses show that large-scale training data, systematic evaluation, and explicit cross-view alignment are all critical for advancing MLLMs from single-view perception toward real-world spatial intelligence. The project page is available at https://github.com/Thinkirin/Crossview-Suite.

</details>


### [233] [SPIKE: An Adaptive Dual Controller Framework for Cost-Efficient Long-Horizon Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18636)
*Wencan Jiang,Jiangning Zhang,Jianbiao Mei,Jinzhuo Liu,Yu Yang,Xiaobin Hu,Zhucun Xue,Yong Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPIKE是一种面向开放世界游戏的自适应双控制器框架，通过事件触发在低速策略控制器和高速反应控制器之间智能切换，并配合分层记忆系统，在Lite-100任务上成功率提升38.5%，同时降低54.9%的token消耗和40.8%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程多模态智能体在开放世界游戏中面临token和延迟预算紧张的问题，频繁的逐步骤推理成本高昂，而纯粹的反应式执行又容易发生漂移、重复失败且恢复能力差。需要一种能够在推理成本与执行稳定性之间取得平衡的方法。

Method: 提出SPIKE框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 双控制器架构——低频全局规划与故障分析的策略控制器和快速本地执行的反应控制器；(2) 事件触发器——基于视觉变化、任务进度、重复动作和失败信号判断何时需要升级到策略推理；(3) 分层记忆——短期经验复用的状态-动作记忆库（SA-MB）与结构化知识图谱（SA-KG），为不同控制器提供所需上下文。

Result: 在StarDojo的Lite-100基准上，SPIKE在成功率上提升5.0个百分点（相对提升38.5%），在预算约束下的成功率提升9.3个百分点（相对提升75.6%），同时token消耗降低54.9%，延迟降低40.8%。

Conclusion: 事件触发机制、反应式覆盖和异构记忆都有助于任务成功和故障恢复，支持"选择性推理"而非"每一步都推理"的设计理念，能有效降低计算成本同时保持高成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon multimodal agents in open-world games must stay goal-directed across many low-level interactions under tight token and latency budgets. Existing approaches often trade off costly per-step reasoning against reactive execution that can drift, repeat failures, and recover poorly. Our key idea is to reuse strategic reasoning across locally stable segments and reinvoke it at event boundaries. We present SPIKE, an adaptive dual controller framework for cost-efficient long-horizon game control. Its Strategic Controller performs low-frequency global planning, failure analysis, and recovery, while its Reactive Controller handles fast local execution under a strict token budget. An Event Trigger monitors visual change, task progress, repeated actions, and failure signals to decide when control should stay reactive or escalate to strategic reasoning. Hierarchical Memory separates short-term experience reuse in the State-Action Memory Bank (SA-MB) from structured evidence in the State Action Knowledge Graph (SA-KG), allowing each controller to retrieve the context it needs. This design reuses strategic proposals over multiple reactive steps, supports local override when plans become stale, and reserves expensive reasoning for moments where extra deliberation is useful. On the Lite-100 split of StarDojo, SPIKE improves Lite-100 success rate (SR) by 5.0 percentage points (38.5% relative) over the strongest Lite-100 baseline and Budgeted SR by 9.3 points (75.6% relative) over the strongest budgeted baseline. It also reduces token consumption by 54.9% and latency by 40.8%. Ablations show that event triggering, reactive override, and heterogeneous memory each contribute to success and recovery, supporting selective reasoning rather than reasoning at every step.

</details>


### [234] [Leveraging Latent Visual Reasoning in Silence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18641)
*Dongyao Zhu,Zhen Wang,Xi Xiao,Han Jiang,Saeed Vahidian,Wei-Lun Chao,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Yu Su,Raju Vatsavai,Jianyang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了潜在视觉推理在多模态推理中的必要性，发现移除潜在token对性能影响很小。作者提出用注意力奖励机制在强化学习中促进潜在token与文本token的交互，实验表明该方法能提升视觉感知和推理能力，即使推理时很少生成潜在token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在视觉推理方法在推理时会生成连续潜在token，但其必要性不明确。作者质疑：如果去掉这些潜在token性能不下降，它们是否还有意义？

Method: 提出基于注意力的奖励机制，在强化学习阶段鼓励生成的潜在token与后续文本token进行交互，从而提升潜在token的利用率，同时保留纯文本推理的灵活性。

Result: 该方法在多个感知和视觉推理基准上提升了性能，即使后训练后潜在token很少生成，模型依然能表现出更好的视觉grounding和更准确的文本推理能力。

Conclusion: 潜在视觉推理的价值不在于推理时是否显式生成潜在token，而在于它能否在训练阶段隐式地引导模型学习更好的视觉表示和推理能力。

Abstract: Latent visual reasoning involves visual evidence more directly in multimodal reasoning by inserting continuous latent tokens before textual generation. However, the necessity of these latent tokens at inference remains ambiguous. We show that replacing latent tokens with random noise or removing them completely causes little performance degradation across spatial reasoning benchmarks. Reinforcement learning further diminishes the latent generation behavior after post-training. These observations raise a central question: Is latent visual reasoning still meaningful? We argue that its value should be measured by how effectively latent tokens guide learning, rather than whether they persist as an inference-time format. Our analysis shows that latent reasoning is unevenly favorable across question types, yet hard task-level routing for applying latent generation is brittle. Motivated by these findings, we propose an attention-based reward that encourages generated latent tokens to interact with later text tokens during RL. This reward promotes latent utilization when the latent mode is activated while preserving the flexibility to use pure-text reasoning. Experiments show that our method improves performance across perception and visual reasoning benchmarks, even when latent tokens are rarely generated after post-training. Our results highlight that, without explicit expression at inference, latent visual reasoning can shape better visual grounding and more accurate textual reasoning in silence. Our code and trained models are publicly available at \href{https://github.com/ddydyd32/silent-lvr/tree/master}{GitHub} and \href{https://huggingface.co/collections/cornuHGF/silent-lvr}{Hugging Face}.

</details>


### [235] [Better Together: Evaluating the Complementarity of Earth Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18667)
*Thijs L van der Plas,Jacob JW Bakermans,Vishal Nedungadi,Gabrielė Tijūnaitytė,Marc Rußwurm,Ioannis N Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入互补性指数（ECI），评估地球嵌入模型的融合增益而非单独性能。实验表明，融合嵌入在4/6任务中优于最佳单模型，证实单独评估会低估模型能力，且互补性具有任务和位置依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球嵌入模型评估仅关注单模型在下游任务中的性能，忽略了空间对齐嵌入可融合提供更丰富信息的潜力，无法全面反映模型的实际能力。

Method: 提出嵌入互补性指数（ECI），量化融合嵌入相比最佳单模型的性能增益。评估四种模型（AlphaEarth, Tessera, GeoCLIP, SatCLIP）在隔离、成对及联合设置下在六个下游任务中的表现。

Result: 融合嵌入在4/6任务中优于最佳单模型；互补性具有任务和位置依赖性；土地覆盖回归任务中，互补性部分由土地覆盖类别的空间尺度决定。

Conclusion: 未来地球嵌入的最大进展可能不来自单个模型，而是来自模型的组合使用。

Abstract: Earth embedding models transform Earth observation data into embeddings uniquely tied to locations on the Earth's surface. These models are typically evaluated in isolation, comparing the downstream task performance across different Earth embeddings. However, spatially aligned embeddings can naturally be fused, providing richer information per location, a capability that isolated evaluations fail to capture. We therefore propose assessing Earth embeddings by their complementarity: the performance gain of fused embeddings over the best single-model baseline. To operationalise this, we introduce an embedding complementarity index applicable to any embedding and task, and evaluate four Earth embedding models (AlphaEarth, Tessera, GeoCLIP, SatCLIP) in isolation, in all pairs, and jointly across six downstream tasks. Fused embeddings outperform the best single model in four out of six tasks, confirming that single-embedding evaluations often underestimate Earth embedding capabilities. Complementarity proves both task- and location-dependent. Further, for a land cover regression task, we find that complementarity is partially determined by the spatial scale of land cover classes. Complementarity reframes Earth embeddings: the greatest future gains may come not from any single Earth embedding model, but from combinations that are better together.

</details>


### [236] [Lance: Unified Multimodal Modeling by Multi-Task Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18678)
*Fengyi Fu,Mengqi Huang,Shaojin Wu,Yunsheng Jiang,Yufei Huo,Hao Li,Yinghang Song,Fei Ding,Jianzhu Guo,Qian He,Zheren Fu,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lance是一个轻量级原生统一多模态模型，通过协作多任务训练和双流混合专家架构，在图像和视频的理解、生成与编辑任务上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型过度依赖模型规模扩展或文本-图像主导设计，缺乏轻量级且高效的原生统一方案。

Method: 采用双流混合专家架构（dual-stream MoE）处理共享交错多模态序列，引入模态感知旋转位置编码（modality-aware RoPE），并采用分阶段多任务训练策略与自适应数据调度。

Result: 在图像和视频生成任务上显著优于现有开源统一模型，同时保持强大的多模态理解能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级原生统一多模态模型可以通过协作多任务训练与解耦能力路径设计实现高效的理解与生成统一。

Abstract: We present Lance, a lightweight native unified model supporting multimodal understanding, generation, and editing for both images and videos. Rather than relying on model capacity scaling or text-image-dominant designs, Lance explores a practical paradigm for unified multimodal modeling via collaborative multi-task training. It is grounded in two core principles: unified context modeling and decoupled capability pathways. Specifically, Lance is trained from scratch and employs a dual-stream mixture-of-experts architecture on shared interleaved multimodal sequences, enabling joint context learning while decoupling the pathways for understanding and generation. We further introduce modality-aware rotary positional encoding to mitigate interference among heterogeneous visual tokens and boost cross-task alignment. During training, Lance adopts a staged multi-task training paradigm with capability-oriented objectives and adaptive data scheduling to strengthen both semantic comprehension and visual generation performance. Experimental results demonstrate that Lance substantially outperforms existing open-source unified models in image and video generation, while retaining strong multimodal understanding capabilities. The homepage is available at https://lance-project.github.io.

</details>


### [237] [CMAG: Concept-Scaffolded Retrieval for Marketplace Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18680)
*Rajeev Goel,Jason Ding,Phani Harish Wajjala,Pavan Turaga,Tejaswi Gowda,Krishna C. Garikipati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CMAG框架，通过3D概念支架、视图感知部件发现、分类路由器和智能体视觉语言模型，实现从离散3D资产中根据文本提示检索并组装风格一致、拓扑兼容的虚拟形象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元宇宙平台依赖分类标签化的3D资产市场，但用户自由文本检索存在歧义、元数据噪声、组件风格不协调和几何不兼容等问题。

Method: 1) 合成中间3D概念支架以消除文本歧义；2) 视图感知部件发现模块进行提示分解和文本引导分割；3) 提示条件分类路由器解决语义-分类不匹配；4) 混合分类检索器结合部件融合与概念残差回退；5) 智能体视觉语言模型进行过滤、重排序和迭代验证组装。

Result: 在多样化的组合提示上相比强基线方法，检索鲁棒性和组合正确性均有提升。

Conclusion: 3D概念支架在提示歧义场景下对提升检索效果至关重要。

Abstract: Metaverse platforms rely on creator-driven marketplaces where avatars are assembled from discrete, taxonomy-labeled 3D assets (e.g., tops, bottoms, shoes, accessories) under strict category and topology constraints. While users increasingly expect free-form text control, text-only retrieval is brittle: natural language is ambiguous with respect to platform taxonomies, metadata is often noisy or informal, and independently retrieved components can be stylistically inconsistent or geometrically incompatible. We propose \textbf{CMAG}, a concept-scaffolded retrieval and verified composition framework for marketplace avatar generation. Given a prompt, CMAG first synthesizes an intermediate 3D concept scaffold that disambiguates intent beyond text by providing global spatial and stylistic context. In parallel, a view-aware part discovery module extracts localized visual evidence via prompt decomposition and text-grounded segmentation. A prompt-conditioned taxonomy router enforces category coverage and resolves semantic-to-taxonomic mismatch, after which a hybrid category-wise retriever combines part-based fusion with a concept-residual fallback using feature suppression. Finally, an agentic vision--language model filters and re-ranks candidates across categories and drives an iterative verification loop to assemble prompt-faithful, topologically consistent avatars from catalog assets. We evaluate CMAG on diverse compositional prompts and demonstrate improved retrieval robustness and compositional correctness compared to strong baselines, highlighting the importance of 3D concept scaffolding under prompt ambiguity.

</details>


### [238] [SafeDiffusion-R1: Online Reward Steering for Safe Diffusion Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18719)
*Komal Kumar,Ankan Deria,Abhishek Basu,Fahad Shamshad,Hisham Cholakkal,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线强化学习（GRPO）的安全扩散模型框架，无需昂贵的监督数据，通过CLIP嵌入的转向奖励机制消除有害内容，同时避免灾难性遗忘，在减少不安全内容和提升生成质量上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖昂贵的监督数据（不安全文本-安全图像对或正负图像对），且离线强化学习或监督微调方法存在灾难性遗忘问题，导致生成质量下降，难以规模化应用。

Method: 采用在线策略的GRPO方法，在正负文本提示上进行后训练；提出转向奖励机制，利用CLIP嵌入的固有属性——将文本表示向正安全方向拉动、远离负安全方向，无需专门的安全/不安全奖励模型。

Result: 不安全内容从48.9%降至18.07%，裸体检测从646降至15，GenEval组合生成质量从42.08%提升至47.83%，且在7种危害类别上均泛化至域外不安全提示。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需监督配对数据或奖励模型微调的情况下，同时实现了安全性和生成质量的提升，具备良好的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have been widely studied for removing unsafe content learned during pre-training. Existing methods require expensive supervised data, either unsafe-text paired with safe-image groundtruth or negative/positive image pairs, making them impractical to scale. Furthermore, offline reinforcement learning and supervised fine-tuning approaches that generate synthetic data offline suffer from catastrophic forgetting, degrading generation quality. We propose a novel online reinforcement learning framework that addresses both data scarcity and model degradation through post-training with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on both negative and positive text prompts. To eliminate the need for fine-tuning specialized safe/unsafe reward models, we introduce a \textit{steering reward mechanism} that exploits an inherent property of CLIP embeddings: steering text representations toward positive safety directions and away from negative ones in the embedding space. Our online-policy approach enables the model to learn from diverse prompts, including explicit unsafe content, without catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method reduces inappropriate content to 18.07\% (vs. 48.9\% for SD v1.4) and nudity detections to 15 (vs. 646 baseline) while improving compositional generation quality from 42.08\% to 47.83\% on GenEval. Remarkably, these safety gains generalize to out-of-domain unsafe prompts across seven harm categories, achieving state-of-the-art performance without supervised paired data or reward tuning. Github: https://github.com/MAXNORM8650/SafeDiffusion-R1.

</details>


### [239] [Advancing Narrative Long Video Generation via Training-Free Identity-Aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18733)
*Jinzhuo Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Wencan Jiang,Yabiao Wang,Dingkang Liang,Zhucun Xue,Ran Yi,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IAMFlow，一种无需训练的身份感知记忆框架，通过LLM提取实体并分配全局ID、VLM异步验证属性，实现多提示下视频生成的一致性，并在新基准NarraStream-Bench上超越最强基线2.56分，同时实现1.39倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成方法在长时一致性和记忆衰减方面存在严重问题，尤其是面对不断演变的提示和实体引用变化时，会出现身份漂移、角色重复和属性丢失。已有方法要么使用预定义策略压缩历史帧，要么基于粗糙的隐式注意力信号检索关键帧，均无法有效处理实体身份的持续跟踪。

Method: 提出IAMFlow框架，包含三部分：1) LLM从提示中提取实体及其视觉属性并分配全局唯一ID；2) VLM异步验证并从渲染帧中精炼属性，实现显式实体追踪；3) 系统级推理加速流水线，包括异步视觉验证、自适应提示转换和模型量化。

Result: 在NarraStream-Bench上，IAMFlow超越最强基线2.56分；在60秒多提示设置下，比最高效基线快1.39倍。

Conclusion: IAMFlow作为无需训练的方法，在视频生成一致性和效率上均达到最优，验证了显式身份跟踪机制的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation has improved rapidly in visual fidelity and interactivity, but it still suffers from long-term inconsistency and memory degradation. Most existing solutions either compress historical frames using predefined strategies or retrieve keyframes based on coarse implicit attention signals, both of which fail to handle evolving prompts with shifting entity references, leading to identity drift, character duplication, and attribute loss. To address this, we propose IAMFlow, a training-free identity-aware memory framework that explicitly models and tracks persistent entity identities, enabling consistent generation across prompt transitions. Specifically, an LLM extracts entities with visual attributes from each prompt and assigns unique global IDs for identity-aware memory, while a VLM asynchronously verifies and refines attributes from rendered frames, enabling explicit entity tracking in place of implicit similarity-based matching. To keep the proposed framework computationally practical, we design a systematic inference acceleration pipeline, including asynchronous visual verification, adaptive prompt transition, and model quantization, which achieves faster generation than existing baselines. Furthermore, we introduce NarraStream-Bench, a benchmark for narrative streaming video generation that features 324 multi-prompt scripts spanning six dimensions and a three-dimensional evaluation protocol that integrates both traditional metrics and multimodal large language model-based assessments. Extensive experiments show that IAMFlow, despite being training-free, achieves the best overall performance on NarraStream-Bench, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.56 points, while achieving a 1.39$\times$ speedup over the most efficient baseline in the 60-second multi-prompt setting.

</details>


### [240] [EgoExoMem: Cross-View Memory Reasoning over Synchronized Egocentric and Exocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18734)
*Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Shaofang Quan,Chengzhi Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Kailun Yang,Kunyu Peng,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个跨视角记忆推理基准EgoExoMem（同步第一/第三人称视频），以及无需训练的帧选择方法E²-Select，实验表明现有模型仅55.3%准确率，该方法达58.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心记忆仅依赖第一人称视角，缺乏全面时空推理能力，受人类从场域视角和观察者视角回忆的启发，需要构建跨视角记忆推理基准。

Method: 构建EgoExoMem基准（2.6K多选问答，覆盖8种时空/跨视角题型）；提出E²-Select，一种无需训练的帧选择方法，结合相关性预算分配与每视角k-DPP采样，处理视角不对称性和跨视角时序一致性。

Result: 最佳多模态大模型仅55.3%；E²-Select达58.2% SOTA；发现系统性的视角偏好冲突。

Conclusion: 自我和外部视角提供互补记忆线索，跨视角记忆推理具有新颖性和挑战性，现有方法仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Egocentric memory is widely used in embodied intelligence, but it may be insufficient for comprehensive spatial-temporal reasoning. Inspired by human recall from both field and observer perspectives, we introduce EgoExoMem, the first benchmark for cross-view memory reasoning over synchronized egocentric and exocentric videos. EgoExoMem contains $2.6K$ high-quality MCQs across eight temporal, spatial, and cross-view QA types. To support dual-view retrieval, we propose E$^2$-Select, a training-free frame selection method for synchronized ego-exo videos. It combines relevance-based budget allocation with per-view k-DPP sampling to handle view asymmetry and cross-view temporal consistency. Experiments show that ego and exo views provide complementary memory cues, while existing MLLMs remain far from solving the benchmark: the best model reaches only $55.3\%$. E$^2$-Select achieves state-of-the-art performance of $58.2\%$ over frame-selection and RAG-based memory baselines. Further analysis reveals systematic view-preference conflicts between question framing and answer grounding, underscoring the novelty and challenge of cross-view memory reasoning.

</details>


### [241] [PIXLRelight: Controllable Relighting via Intrinsic Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18735)
*Miguel Farinha,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIXLRelight是一种前馈式单图像重光照方法，通过结合物理基础渲染和神经渲染，在不到0.1秒内实现高质量、物理可控的重光照效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么光照控制有限（如通过文本或环境贴图），要么在链接逆向和正向渲染时累积误差，要么需要昂贵的逐图像优化。作者希望实现一种既物理可控又高效的单图像重光照方法。

Method: 训练时将多光照照片分解为反照率、漫反射着色和非漫反射残差作为条件；推理时从粗3D重建的路径追踪渲染中计算相同条件；使用基于transformer的神经渲染器，通过逐像素仿射调制应用目标光照，保留图像细节。

Result: 支持任意PBR风格的光照控制，达到最先进的重光照质量，每张图像处理时间不到0.1秒。

Conclusion: PIXLRelight通过共享内在条件桥接PBR和神经渲染，实现了高效、高质量的物理可控单图像重光照。

Abstract: We present PIXLRelight, a feed-forward approach for physically controllable single-image relighting. Existing methods either provide limited lighting control (e.g. through text or environment maps), accumulate errors when chaining inverse and forward rendering, or require costly per-image optimization. Our key idea is to bridge physically based rendering (PBR) and learned image synthesis through a shared intrinsic conditioning that can be obtained from either real photographs or PBR renders. At training time, paired multi-illumination photographs are decomposed into albedo, diffuse shading, and non-diffuse residuals, which condition the model. At inference time, the same conditioning is computed from a path-traced render of a coarse 3D reconstruction of the input under user-specified PBR lights. A transformer-based neural renderer then applies the target illumination to the source photograph, preserving fine image detail through a per-pixel affine modulation. PIXLRelight enables arbitrary PBR-style lighting control, achieves state-of-the-art relighting quality, and runs in under a tenth of a second per image. Code and models are available at https://mlfarinha.github.io/pixl-relight/.

</details>


### [242] [Spectral Progressive Diffusion for Efficient Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18736)
*Howard Xiao,Brian Chao,Lior Yariv,Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出谱渐进扩散框架，利用扩散模型在频域中自回归生成的特性，沿去噪轨迹渐进提升分辨率，实现高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在频域中低频先于高频生成，高频计算在噪声主导阶段存在冗余，现有方法未能有效利用这一结构特性实现高效生成。

Method: 提出Spectral Progressive Diffusion框架，包含谱噪声扩展机制和基于模型功率谱的最优分辨率调度策略，支持免训练加速和微调方案。

Result: 在多个最先进的图像和视频生成预训练模型上取得显著加速，同时保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过利用扩散模型频域生成特性，渐进式分辨率增长能够在保证生成质量的前提下有效提升生成效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models have been shown to implicitly generate visual content autoregressively in the frequency domain, where low-frequency components are generated earlier in the denoising process while high-frequency details emerge only in later timesteps. This structure offers a natural opportunity for efficient generation, as high-resolution computation on noise-dominated frequencies is largely redundant. We propose Spectral Progressive Diffusion, a general framework that progressively grows resolution along the denoising trajectory of pretrained diffusion models. To this end, we develop a spectral noise expansion mechanism and derive an optimal resolution schedule from the model's power spectrum. Our framework supports training-free acceleration and a novel fine-tuning recipe that further improves efficiency and quality. We demonstrate significant speedups on state-of-the-art pretrained image and video generation models while preserving visual quality.

</details>


### [243] [LongLive-2.0: An NVFP4 Parallel Infrastructure for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18739)
*Yukang Chen,Luozhou Wang,Wei Huang,Shuai Yang,Bohan Zhang,Yicheng Xiao,Ruihang Chu,Weian Mao,Qixin Hu,Shaoteng Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Huizi Mao,Ying-Cong Chen,Enze Xie,Xiaojuan Qi,Song Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongLive-2.0提出了基于NVFP4的并行基础设施，支持长视频生成的训练和推理全流程，通过序列并行自回归训练、NVFP4精度优化和高效推理策略，实现训练2.15倍、推理1.84倍的加速，在5B模型上达到45.7 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成面临训练和推理中的速度和内存瓶颈，现有方法依赖复杂的ODE初始化和分布匹配蒸馏，缺乏高效的并行基础设施和精度优化方案。

Method: 1) 提出Balanced SP序列并行自回归训练，结合clean-history和noisy-target分块实现教师强制掩码；2) 采用NVFP4精度降低GPU内存并加速GEMM计算；3) 直接微调扩散模型为长序列自回归扩散模型，避免复杂蒸馏；4) 推理端支持W4A4 NVFP4推理、KV Cache量化、异步流式VAE解码；5) 非Blackwell架构上部署SP推理。

Result: 训练加速2.15倍，推理加速1.84倍，LongLive-2.0-5B实现45.7 FPS的推理速度，在多个基准测试上表现优异。

Conclusion: LongLive-2.0是首个支持NVFP4训练和推理的长视频生成系统，证明了高质量基础设施和数据集可以简化训练流程，实现高效的端到端长视频生成。

Abstract: We present LongLive-2.0, an NVFP4-based parallel infrastructure throughout the full training and inference workflow of long video generation, addressing speed and memory bottlenecks. For training, we introduce sequence-parallel autoregressive (AR) training, instantiated as Balanced SP, which co-designs the efficient teacher-forcing layout with SP execution by pairing clean-history and noisy-target temporal chunks on each rank, enabling a natural teacher-forcing mask with SP-aware chunked VAE encoding. Combined with NVFP4 precision, it reduces GPU memory cost and accelerates GEMM computation during training, the proportion of which increases as video length grows. Moreover, we show that a high-quality infrastructure and dataset enable a remarkably clean training pipeline. Unlike existing Self-Forcing series methods that rely on ODE initialization and subsequent distribution matching distillation (DMD), LongLive-2.0 directly tunes a diffusion model into a long, multi-shot, interactive auto-regressive (AR) diffusion model. It can be further converted to real-time generation (4 to 2 denoising steps) with standalone LoRA weights. For inference on Blackwell GPUs, we enable W4A4 NVFP4 inference, quantize KV cache into NVFP4 for memory savings, and boost end-to-end throughput with asynchronous streaming VAE decoding. On non-Blackwell GPU architectures, we deploy SP inference to match the speed on Blackwell GPUs, while the quantized KV cache can lower inter-GPU communication of SP. Experiments show up to 2.15x speedup in training, and 1.84x in inference. LongLive-2.0-5B achieves 45.7 FPS inference while attaining strong performance on benchmarks. To our knowledge, LongLive-2.0 is the first NVFP4 training and inference system for long video generation.

</details>


### [244] [Aurora: Unified Video Editing with a Tool-Using Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18748)
*Yongsheng Yu,Ziyun Zeng,Zhiyuan Xiao,Zhenghong Zhou,Hang Hua,Wei Xiong,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Aurora是一个将工具增强的VLM智能体与统一视频扩散Transformer结合的框架，能自动解析用户模糊需求并规划编辑方案，在视频编辑任务上超越仅靠指令的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一视频编辑模型虽然灵活，但假设用户已提供模型可直接使用的文本、参考图像和局部编辑的空间定位信息，而真实用户请求往往缺乏这些明确信息，导致文本和视觉上的欠指定问题。

Method: 提出Aurora框架，核心包括：(1) 工具增强的VLM智能体，将用户原始请求映射为与扩散Transformer条件通道对齐的结构化编辑计划；(2) 对VLM智能体进行监督训练（含完整编辑规划和参考图像选择）和偏好训练（用于鲁棒工具使用和指令优化）；(3) 引入AgentEdit-Bench基准评估智能体增强的视频编辑。

Result: 在AgentEdit-Bench和两个现有视频编辑基准上，Aurora优于仅依赖指令的基线方法，且VLM智能体可迁移到其他兼容的冻结视频编辑模型。

Conclusion: Aurora通过智能体感知的编辑规划有效解决了视频编辑中用户请求的欠指定问题，提升了编辑质量和用户友好性，且具有良好的模型迁移性。

Abstract: Recent video editing models have converged on a unified conditioning design: a single diffusion transformer jointly consumes text, source video, and reference images, and one set of weights covers replacement, removal, style transfer, and reference-driven insertion. The design is flexible, but it assumes that the user already provides model-ready text, reference images, and spatial grounding for local edits, which real requests often omit. We present Aurora, an agentic video editing framework that pairs a tool-augmented vision-language model (VLM) agent with a unified video diffusion transformer. The VLM agent maps a raw user request to a structured edit plan aligned with the transformer's conditioning channels, thereby resolving textual and visual underspecification before generation. We train the VLM agent with supervised data for complete edit planning and reference-image selection, together with preference pairs for robust tool use and instruction refinement. We introduce AgentEdit-Bench to evaluate agent-enhanced video editing under textual and visual underspecification. Experiments on AgentEdit-Bench and two existing video editing benchmarks show that Aurora improves over instruction-only baselines and that the VLM agent transfers to compatible frozen video editing models. Project page: https://yeates.github.io/Aurora-Page

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [The Scaling Laws of Skills in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16508)
*Charles Chen,Qiming Yu,Yuhang Gu,Zhuoye Huang,Hanjing Li,Hongyu Liu,Simin Liu,Jinhao Liu,Dengyun Peng,Jiangyi Wang,Zheng Yan,Fanqing Meng,Ethan Qin,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现智能体技能库存在两个耦合的标度定律：路由精度随库大小对数衰减（路由定律），而正确执行可将下游决策提升约4倍（执行定律）。通过一个参数（路由对数衰减斜率b）连接两个定律，并据此优化将路由准确率从71.3%提升至91.7%，劫持率从22.4%降至4.1%，下游执行通过率提升12.3-6.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统规模扩大时技能库不断增长，但技能库的标度规律（scaling laws）尚不明确，缺乏对路由和执行行为的系统性理解。

Method: 在15个前沿LLM、1,141个真实技能和超过300万个路由/执行决策上，系统分析技能库规模对路由准确率和执行成功率的影响，识别出两个耦合标度定律，并通过一个共享参数连接二者。

Result: 路由定律：单步路由准确率随库大小对数衰减（R²>0.97），错误从局部技能竞争发展到跨族漂移和"黑洞技能"捕获。执行定律：联合路由近似乘法，正确执行可使下游困难决策提升约4倍。参数b连接两定律。优化后路由准确率从71.3%提升至91.7%，劫持率从22.4%降至4.1%，ClawBench通过率从49.3%提升至61.6%，ClawMark从28.4%提升至34.5%。

Conclusion: 智能体性能不仅取决于模型能力，还依赖于技能库的结构、粒度和暴露策略，这些定律可用于指导实际优化。

Abstract: As agent systems scale, skills accumulate into large reusable libraries, yet their scaling laws remain poorly understood. Across 15 frontier LLMs, 1,141 real-world skills, and over 3M routing or execution decisions, we identify two coupled laws. Routing law: single-step routing accuracy decays logarithmically with library size ($R^2{>}0.97$ for all models), with errors progressing from local skill competition to cross-family drift and capture by overly general "black-hole skills". Execution law: before state realization, joint routing is approximately multiplicative, whereas correct execution can improve difficult downstream decisions by about $4{\times}$. A single parameter, the routing logarithmic decay slope $b$, couples the two laws: routing-side fits predict execution-side rescue across models, showing that the same library property controls both pre-execution collapse and downstream recoverability. The laws are actionable: law-guided optimization raises held-out routing accuracy from 71.3% to 91.7%, reduces hijack from 22.4% to 4.1%, and transfers directionally to downstream ClawBench and ClawMark execution settings, improving mean pass rate from 49.3% to 61.6% on ClawBench and from 28.4% to 34.5% on ClawMark. These results show that agent performance depends not only on model capability, but also on the structure, granularity, and exposure policy of the skill library.

</details>


### [246] [PQR: A Framework to Generate Diverse and Realistic User Queries that Elicit QA Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16551)
*Yunan Lu,Luigi Liu,Omar Yahia,Arpit Sharma,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PQR是一个通过双模块迭代交互自动生成既真实又能触发智能体失败行为的查询框架，在电商QA场景中比先前方法多发现23%-78%的无帮助响应，且查询更多样真实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注对抗性用户触发的智能体失败，忽视了那些包含真实用户意图但同样导致智能体失败的查询。识别有意义的失败案例需要大量人工设计测试场景，成本高昂。

Method: PQR框架包含两个互补模块的迭代交互：查询精炼模块探索多样化的查询变体，提示精炼模块利用先前反馈推导新的违反目标和现实主义策略，从而生成既触发失败又真实的查询。

Result: 在电商QA智能体检测中，PQR比先前方法多发现23%-78%的无帮助响应，生成的查询在多样性和真实性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PQR框架能有效发现针对特定目标（如有帮助性、安全性等）的智能体失败，同时保持查询的真实性，为评估LLM智能体提供了一种更高效、更全面的自动化方法。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM-based agents remains challenging because identifying meaningful failure cases often requires substantial human effort to design realistic test scenarios. Prior works primarily focus on automatically discovering agent failures induced by adversarial users, while overlooking queries with real user intents that also trigger agent failures. We introduce PQR, a framework that not only surfaces agent failures with respect to specific objectives (e.g., helpfulness, safety, etc.) but also resembles real users' intents. PQR operates through an iterative interaction between two complementary modules. The query refinement module performs rewrites to explore diverse query variations, while the prompt refinement module uses prior feedback to derive new objective-violating strategies and realism policies for refining prompts, which in turn generate failure-triggering yet realistic queries. We evaluate PQR on detecting an e-commerce QA agent's unhelpful responses. Our method uncovers 23% - 78% more unhelpful responses, and our generated queries are more diverse and realistic compared to previous methods.

</details>


### [247] [Scaling Accessible Mathematics on arXiv: HTML Conversion and MathML 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16562)
*Deyan Ginev,Brian Caruso,Bruce Miller,Jeff Sank,Jacob Weiskoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: arXiv HTML论文项目持续发展，2025-2026年主要进展包括：社区驱动的HTML质量改进、语料库规模转换达到75%无错误、MathML 4意图注释支持无障碍语音、以及LaTeXML的Rust移植以降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为arXiv读者提供高质量的HTML格式论文，改善在线阅读体验和可访问性。

Method: 社区反馈驱动的HTML质量改进、大规模语料库转换、MathML 4意图注释、以及将LaTeXML用Rust语言重新实现。

Result: 约一半的6000份用户报告已解决；HTML无错误率从0提升至75%；实现了初始的MathML 4意图注释；Rust移植降低了计算成本并加快了预览速度。

Conclusion: arXiv HTML论文项目仍处于实验阶段，但随着对读者需求和技术机会的深入理解，正在逐步成熟。

Abstract: We report on the ongoing development of arXiv's HTML Papers offering, available on every new TeX/LaTeX submission since its initial release in 2023.
  The main highlights from 2025 and early 2026 are:
  (i) community-driven improvements to HTML fidelity and service health, with roughly half of 6,000 user reports resolved;
  (ii) corpus-scale conversion work aimed at 90% error-free HTML (currently 75%);
  (iii) initial MathML 4 Intent annotations for accessible speech output;
  (iv) an in-progress Rust port of LaTeXML, reducing compute costs and enabling faster previews on submission.
  The arXiv HTML Papers project remains experimental, but is gradually maturing as we better understand the needs of arXiv's readers and the technical opportunities presented by new standards and by advances in programming languages and AI.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Sentiment Classification: A Generative Framework for Emotion Intensity Evaluation in Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16613)
*Francesco A. Fabozzi,Dasol Kim,William N. Goetzmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种从情绪识别转向情绪强度评估的新方法，通过对生成式语言模型进行微调输出0-100的连续值，在金融等领域表现优于传统分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散情绪分类在金融等应用领域存在局限性，无法捕捉情绪的程度差异，需要更细粒度的情绪建模方式。

Method: 构建情绪强度评分数据集，对开源生成式语言模型进行微调，使其输出0-100的连续值来评估情绪强度。

Result: 该方法超越分类基线，展现出良好的泛化能力，并能迁移到情感和唤醒度等相关构念。

Conclusion: 情绪强度评估作为分类的替代方案更能满足金融等领域对情绪程度信息的需求，推动NLP的跨学科重构。

Abstract: We introduce a novel approach to emotion modeling that shifts the focus from
  identification to evaluation, addressing the limitations of discrete classification in
  applied domains such as finance. By constructing a dataset of emotional intensity
  scores and fine-tuning open-weight generative language models to output continuous
  values from 0-100, we demonstrate a more expressive, generalizable framework for
  sentiment and emotion analysis. Our findings not only outperform classification
  baselines but also reveal surprising generalization capabilities and transfer effects
  to related constructs such as sentiment and arousal. This work contributes to the
  interdisciplinary recontextualization of NLP by introducing emotion intensity
  evaluation as an alternative to classification, arguing that this shift better aligns
  with the needs of domains--such as finance--where the degree of emotional content is
  central to interpretation and decision-making.

</details>


### [249] [SKG-Eval: Stateful Evaluation of Multi-Turn Dialogue via Incremental Semantic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16650)
*Avijit Shil,Suman Samui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SKG-Eval，一种基于语义知识图谱的多轮对话评估框架，通过结构化三元组提取和三大信号（局部相关性、历史一致性、逻辑连贯性）来检测长程对话中的矛盾、主题漂移和实体不一致问题，无需依赖NLI模型或LLM评判，具有准确定性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估方法（如LLM-as-a-judge和基于嵌入的指标）多依赖扁平或单轮独立的表示，难以有效检测对话中的长程问题（如矛盾、主题漂移、实体不一致）。

Method: 构建动态语义知识图谱（SKG），通过结构化三元组提取逐步更新图结构，并计算三个互补信号：局部相关性（与当前提示的对齐）、历史一致性（新信息与先前上下文的连接）、逻辑连贯性（基于几何矛盾引擎检测跨轮冲突）。最后通过加权趋势分析聚合为长度不变的会话评分。

Result: 在多个基准上，SKG-Eval与人类判断的相关性更高，显著提升了扩展对话中长程不一致问题的检测能力，并能生成明确的矛盾证书和确定性分数。

Conclusion: 通过语义知识图谱的结构化外部状态追踪，为基于LLM的对话评估提供了一种可扩展的隐式推理替代方案，支持可复现和可审计的评估。

Abstract: Evaluating multi-turn dialogue systems remains challenging because response quality depends not only on the current prompt, but also on previously established entities, claims, and conversational commitments. Existing automatic evaluators, including LLM-as-a-judge frameworks and embedding-based metrics, largely rely on flat or turn-isolated representations, making them less effective at detecting long-range issues such as contradiction, topic drift, and entity inconsistency. To address this, we propose SKG-Eval, a quasi-deterministic and interpretable framework that models dialogue as an evolving Semantic Knowledge Graph (SKG) of entities, relations, and commitments across turns. The framework incrementally updates the graph through structured triple extraction and computes three complementary signals: (i) local relevance, measuring alignment with the current prompt and optional reference; (ii) historical consistency, evaluating how newly introduced information connects to prior conversational context using graph-based and embedding-driven signals; and (iii) logical coherence, assessed by a geometric contradiction engine that detects cross-turn conflicts without relying on NLI models or LLM judges. These signals are adaptively fused and aggregated into a length-invariant session score via recency-weighted trend analysis. Across multiple benchmarks, SKG-Eval achieves higher correlation with human judgments and substantially improves detection of long-range inconsistencies in extended conversations. In addition, the framework produces explicit contradiction certificates and deterministic scores for fixed inputs, enabling reproducible and auditable evaluation. Overall, our results suggest that structured externalized state tracking through semantic knowledge graphs provides a scalable alternative to implicit reasoning in LLM-based dialogue evaluators.

</details>


### [250] [A Scalable Tool for Measuring Manner and Result Verbs in Developmental Language Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16654)
*Divyesh Pratap Singh,Dakshesh Gusain,Federica Bulgarelli,Alison Eisel Hendricks,John Beavers,Nathan M. Beers,Ifeoma Nwogu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用大语言模型生成标注，训练RoBERTa分类器，实现了在句子层面以89.6%准确率识别方式动词和结果动词，为发展语言学中的动词语义研究提供了可扩展的测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方式和结果动词编码了事件结构的不同方面，对研究早期动词学习具有重要意义，但目前缺乏大规模标注资源来支持这一区分在规模上的测量。

Method: 基于语言学启发的提示，利用大语言模型生成句子级标注，覆盖来自MASC和InterCorp的数据，将VerbNet的标注类别扩展到436类；随后在此标注上训练RoBERTa分类器。

Result: 在三个保留的黄金标准数据集上评估，模型平均准确率达到89.6%，表现良好。

Conclusion: 该工作为发展语言学及其他语言数据集中的动词语义研究提供了可扩展的测量工具，但指出边界案例、混合方式/结果动词以及下游发展应用仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Manner and result verbs encode different aspects of event structure and have been discussed in developmental work as a potentially informative distinction for studying early verb learning. However, this distinction remains difficult to measure at scale because large annotated resources for manner and result classification are not currently available. We present a computational approach for identifying manner and result verbs in sentence context. Using linguistically informed prompts, we generate sentence-level annotations with large language models over data drawn from MASC and InterCorp, extending coverage from previously annotated portions of VerbNet to 436 classes. We then train a RoBERTa-based classifier on these annotations and evaluate it on three held-out gold-standard datasets, including previously annotated items and a new expert-annotated set. Across these evaluations, the model shows promising performance, with average accuracy up to 89.6%. We present this work as a scalable measurement tool that can support future research on verb semantics in developmental and other language datasets, while noting that further validation is needed for borderline cases, mixed manner/result verbs, and downstream developmental applications.

</details>


### [251] [Language Acquisition Device in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16758)
*Masato Mita,Taiga Someya,Ryo Yoshida,Yohei Oseki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出受语言习得机制（LAD）启发的大语言模型预预训练（PPT）方法，使用形式语言MP-STRUCT。该语言通过MERGE、AGREE、MOVE编码层次组合、特征依赖和长距离位移。仅500步PPT即可匹配强基线模型的token效率，同时赋予模型对结构不合理语言（如REVERSE）的人类式抗性。研究发现，MP-STRUCT CORE虽在C-RASP中不可定义（超越transformer形式表达能力上限），却优于k-Shuffle Dyck，挑战了“有效PPT语言必须兼具层次表达性与电路可学习性”的假设。功能地标（降低依赖解析歧义）是关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数据效率上远低于人类，受语言习得装置（LAD）假说启发——该假说认为先天约束能预先限制学习者的假设空间到自然语言类似结构——作者希望通过设计具有自然语言核心特征的形式语言进行预预训练，提升LLM的数据效率和结构偏向。

Method: 设计形式语言MP-STRUCT，包含MERGE（层次组合）、AGREE（特征依赖）、MOVE（长距离位移）三种运算；在LLM正式训练前进行500步的预预训练（PPT）；通过消融实验分析各成分贡献，并与k-Shuffle Dyck等基线对比。

Result: 1) 500步MP-STRUCT PPT匹配强形式语言基线token效率；2) 赋予模型对人类不合理的语言（REVERSE）的抗性；3) MP-STRUCT CORE尽管在C-RASP中不可定义（即transformer形式表达力上限之外），仍优于k-Shuffle Dyck。

Conclusion: 有效PPT语言的设计不仅取决于形式表达力，还取决于依赖解析的易访问性；功能地标（降低解析歧义）是关键。挑战了“有效PPT语言必须同时满足层次表达性和电路可学习性”的既有假设。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain substantially less data-efficient than humans. Pre-pretraining (PPT) on synthetic languages has been proposed to close this gap, with prior work emphasizing highly expressive formal languages such as $k$-Shuffle Dyck. Inspired by the Language Acquisition Device (LAD) hypothesis, which posits that innate constraints preemptively restrict the learner's hypothesis space to natural-language-like structure, we propose LAD-inspired PPT: pre-pretraining on MP-STRUCT, a formal language whose strings encode hierarchical composition, feature-based dependencies, and long-distance displacement via MERGE, AGREE, and MOVE. A brief 500-step PPT with MP-STRUCT matches strong formal-language baselines in token efficiency while additionally imparting a human-like resistance to structurally implausible languages (e.g., REVERSE). Analyzing simplified variants, we find that MP-STRUCT CORE outperforms $k$-Shuffle Dyck despite not being definable in C-RASP (a formal bound on transformer expressivity), challenging the prior hypothesis that effective PPT languages must be both hierarchically expressive and circuit-theoretically learnable. We show that functional landmarks, which reduce dependency resolution ambiguity, are a key driver, suggesting that effective PPT design depends not only on expressivity but also on the accessibility of dependency resolution.

</details>


### [252] [Retrieval-Based Multi-Label Legal Annotation: Extensible, Data-Efficient and Hallucination-Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16767)
*Li Zhang,Jaromir Savelka,Kevin Ashley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将多标签法律文本标注转化为检索任务，使用冻结的检索模型嵌入文档和标签描述，通过k近邻预测标签，避免了传统编码器的重新训练和生成式大模型的计算开销与幻觉问题。在三个法律数据集上，该方法在精度、数据效率和计算成本上均优于现有方法，且严格遵循标签集，消除幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多标签法律标注方法存在两大局限：参数化编码器需要针对特定任务训练且标签集变更时必须重新训练；生成式大模型在标签空间增大时性能下降且计算成本高昂。作者希望提出一种无需梯度回传、可灵活更新标签集、高数据效率且能避免幻觉的替代方案。

Method: 将法律标注建模为检索问题：使用冻结的检索模型分别对文档和标签描述进行嵌入，通过k近邻算法在嵌入空间中预测标签。标签集更新时只需重新嵌入和重构索引，无需反向传播训练。

Result: 在ECtHR-A、ECtHR-B和Eurlex三个法律数据集上，检索方法达到竞争性精度和强数据效率。Eurlex上Qwen-8B检索将Macro-F1从40.41（GPT-5.2零样本）提升至49.12，计算成本降低20-30倍。仅用100个训练样本时，ECtHR-A上Micro-F1几乎翻倍（48.29 vs. 27.87）。此外，GPT-5.2在0.12-0.9%的测试样本中产生标签幻觉，而检索方法严格遵循定义标签集。

Conclusion: 基于检索模型的法律标注器在高基数、快速变化的法律标签空间中是一种实用、可部署的替代方案，兼顾精度、效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-label legal annotation requires assigning multiple labels from large, evolving taxonomies to long, fact-intensive documents, often under limited supervision. Parametric encoders typically require task-specific training and retraining when the label set changes, while prompting generative large language models becomes costly and degrades as the label space grows. We cast legal annotation as retrieval: we embed documents and label descriptions with a frozen retrieval model and predict labels via k-nearest neighbors in the embedding space, enabling updates by re-embedding and re-indexing rather than gradient-based backpropagation. Across three legal datasets (ECtHR-A, ECtHR-B, and Eurlex with 100 labels), retrieval achieves competitive accuracy and strong data efficiency; on Eurlex, Qwen-8B retrieval improves Macro-F1 from 40.41 (GPT-5.2, zero-shot) to 49.12 while reducing estimated compute by 20-30 times compared to fine-tuning. With only (N=100) training samples, retrieval nearly doubles Micro-F1 over hierarchical Legal-BERT on ECtHR-A (48.29 vs. 27.87). We also quantify a reliability failure mode of generative inference: GPT-5.2 hallucinates labels outside the provided taxonomy in 0.12-0.9% of test samples under deterministic decoding. In contrast, retrieval strictly respects defined label sets, eliminating hallucination by design. These results suggest retrieval-model-based annotators are a practical, deployable alternative for high-cardinality and rapidly changing legal label spaces.

</details>


### [253] [Exploring Lightweight Large Language Models for Court View Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16770)
*Zhitian Hou,Tianyong Hao,Nanli Zeng,Zhixiong Chao,Kun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统探索了轻量级（<2B参数）大语言模型在刑事法院观点生成（CVG）任务中的能力，构建了CVGEvalKit评估框架，并通过实验分析了模型架构、规模以及任务间影响等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级LLM在司法AI中具有部署成本低、隐私保护好的优势，但此前缺乏对其在法院观点生成任务中能力的系统研究。

Method: 设计了包含4个研究问题的实验方案，构建包含3个公开数据集的CVGEvalKit评估框架，在混合训练集上训练模型并在各测试集上评估。

Result: 实验揭示了模型架构、模型规模与任务间影响之间的权衡关系，验证了轻量级LLM在司法AI中的潜力。

Conclusion: 轻量级LLM在法院观点生成和罪名预测任务中表现出竞争力，为司法AI应用提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Criminal Court View Generation (CVG) is a critical task in Legal Artificial Intelligence (Legal AI), involving the generation of court view based on case facts. In this work, we systematically explore the capabilities of lightweight (smaller than 2B) large language models (LLMs) in CVG and their impact on charge prediction. Our study addresses four key questions: (1) how does different architecture of LLMs affect the CVG quality and charge prediction. (2) how does LLMs size contribute to the performance, (3) how do lightweight LLMs compare with Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) in these tasks, and (4) how does predicting charge by court view generation first compare with predicting it directly. Additionally, we also develop CVGEvalKit, an evaluation framework including three public available datasets for CVG tasks, as well as predicting their charges. Comprehensive experiments are conducted on this framework, where models are trained on a mixed training set and evaluated on each dataset's test set. Experimental results provide new insights into the trade-offs between model architecture, model size, and the influence between different tasks, highlighting the potential of lightweight LLMs in judicial AI applications. The source code is anonymously available at \url{https://github.com/ZhitianHou/CVGEvalKit}

</details>


### [254] [AgentKernelArena: Generalization-Aware Benchmarking of GPU Kernel Optimization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16819)
*Sharareh Younesian,Wenwen Ouyang,Sina Rafati,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Sharon Zhou,Ji Liu,Yue Liu,Yuchen Yang,Hao Li,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Vikram Appia,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了AgentKernelArena，一个用于评估AI编码智能体在GPU内核优化任务上表现的开放基准，涵盖196个任务和三种优化类型，发现最强配置在PyTorch-to-HIP任务上实现最高6.89x加速，并揭示了不同任务类型在未见配置泛化上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU内核基准测试仅评估单次LLM调用，缺乏对完整智能体工作流的评测，且未包含内核到内核优化以及未见配置泛化测试。

Method: 构建包含196个任务的基准，涵盖HIP-to-HIP优化、Triton-to-Triton优化和PyTorch-to-HIP翻译三类任务；采用隔离工作区、门控编译、正确性检查和性能检查评估完整智能体工作流；设计了未见配置泛化协议来测试优化是否迁移到未见过的输入配置。

Result: 最强配置在PyTorch-to-HIP上实现平均6.89x加速，HIP-to-HIP上6.69x加速，Triton-to-Triton上2.13x加速；未见配置测试显示HIP-to-HIP和Triton-to-Triton泛化良好，但PyTorch-to-HIP正确率显著下降。

Conclusion: AgentKernelArena可作为模块化、可扩展的框架，用于严格评估智能体GPU内核优化，并揭示了现有智能体在不同任务类型泛化能力上的显著差异。

Abstract: GPU kernel optimization is increasingly critical for efficient deep learning systems, but writing high-performance kernels still requires substantial low-level expertise. Recent AI coding agents can iteratively read code, invoke compilers and profilers, and refine implementations, yet existing kernel benchmarks evaluate single LLM calls rather than full agent workflows, and none include both kernel-to-kernel optimization and unseen-configuration generalization testing. We present AgentKernelArena, an open-source benchmark for measuring AI coding agents on GPU kernel optimization. The benchmark contains 196 tasks spanning HIP-to-HIP optimization, Triton-to-Triton optimization, and PyTorch-to-HIP translation, and evaluates complete agent workflows in isolated workspaces using gated compilation, correctness, and performance checks, centralized scoring and an unseen-configuration generalization protocol that tests whether optimizations transfer to input configurations the agent never observed. Across production agents including Cursor Agent, Claude Code, and Codex Agent, we find near-perfect compilation and high correctness rates on most task categories, with the strongest configurations achieving mean speedups of up to 6.89x on PyTorch-to-HIP, 6.69x on HIP-to-HIP, and 2.13x on Triton-to-Triton tasks. Our unseen-configuration evaluation shows that HIP-to-HIP and Triton-to-Triton optimizations largely transfer to unseen input shapes, while PyTorch-to-HIP exhibits substantial correctness drops, indicating that agents generating kernels from scratch frequently hardcode shape-specific assumptions. AgentKernelArena is designed as a modular, extensible framework for rigorous evaluation of agentic GPU kernel optimization across agents, tasks, and hardware targets.

</details>


### [255] [Constrained Code Generation with Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16829)
*Lize Shao,Michael Cardei,Zichen Xie,Ferdinando Fioretto,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CDC，一种无需训练的神经符号推理框架，通过在离散扩散模型的去噪过程中直接集成约束满足（功能正确性、安全性、语法），实现更优的代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在代码生成中具备全局程序状态的可干预性，但现有方法缺乏在生成过程中有效整合功能性及安全性约束的手段。

Method: CDC在离散扩散的反向去噪过程中注入约束感知的去噪算子，结合数学优化与程序分析，定位与约束相关的中间程序状态区域，并局部调整去噪轨迹，使生成向可行程序靠拢。

Result: 在多个代码生成基准上，CDC在功能正确性、安全性和语法约束满足方面一致优于离散扩散和自回归基线，且所需纠正计算更少、编辑更局部化。

Conclusion: 无需额外训练的约束引导推理框架能有效提升离散扩散模型在代码生成中的约束满足能力，具备高效、局部化的优势。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are a powerful, emerging paradigm for code generation. They construct programs through iterative refinement of partially corrupted token sequences and enable parallel token refinement. Importantly, this paradigm exposes a global program state at each denoising step, which provides a natural intervention point for enforcing program-level functionality and security constraints, guiding the generation before the final code is committed. Building on this observation, the paper introduces Constrained Diffusion for Code (CDC), a training-free neurosymbolic inference framework that integrates constraint satisfaction directly into the reverse denoising process. CDC augments the base discrete diffusion sampler with constraint-aware denoising operators that combine mathematical optimization with program analysis to identify constraint-relevant regions of the intermediate program state and locally adjust the denoising trajectory, steering generation toward feasible programs while remaining close to the base model. Across code generation benchmarks, CDC consistently improves constraint satisfaction in functional correctness, security, and even syntax, outperforming discrete diffusion and autoregressive baselines with less corrective computation and more localized edits.

</details>


### [256] [CompactAttention: Accelerating Chunked Prefill with Block-Union KV Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16839)
*Jiwon Song,Dongwon Jo,Beomseok Kang,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CompactAttention，一种基于块联合KV选择的块级预填充注意力机制，通过将2D块稀疏掩码转换为GQA感知的逐组KV块表，在不进行显式KV压缩的情况下实现高效注意力计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法主要针对一次性预填充设计，在块级预填充场景下效率低下；而QUOKA虽直接针对块级预填充，但存在基于查询子采样的KV选择可能遗漏查询特定KV条目，并引入显式KV拷贝开销的问题。

Method: 提出Block-Union KV Selection机制，将2D块稀疏掩码作为KV选择信号而非直接稀疏核执行计划，通过Q块联合和组内联合转换为GQA感知的逐组KV块表，在分页执行约束下保留所有被选中的KV块，实现原位访问无需显式KV压缩。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct上，RULER基准测试中保持接近密集注意力的精度，在128K上下文长度的块级预填充下实现高达2.72倍的注意力加速。

Conclusion: CompactAttention通过块联合KV选择策略，有效解决了块级预填充场景下稀疏注意力的效率问题，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算速度。

Abstract: Chunked prefill has become a widely adopted serving strategy for long-context large language models, but efficient attention computation in this regime remains challenging. Existing sparse attention methods are primarily designed for one-shot prefill and do not translate efficiently to chunked prefill: block-sparse kernels lose efficiency when the query length is limited by the chunk size, while fine-grained pattern search becomes costly when repeated over the accumulated KV cache at every chunk. QUOKA, a recent method that directly targets chunked prefill, avoids sparse-kernel overhead but relies on query-subsampled, token-level KV selection, which can miss query-specific KV entries and introduce explicit KV-copy overhead. To address these limitations, we propose CompactAttention, a chunked-prefill attention mechanism based on Block-Union KV Selection. CompactAttention treats 2D block-sparse masks as KV-selection signals rather than direct sparse-kernel execution plans, and converts them into GQA-aware per-group KV block tables through Q-block union and intra-group union. This construction produces the minimal block tables that preserve all KV blocks selected by the input masks under paged execution constraints, enabling selected KV blocks to be accessed in place without explicit KV compaction.
