TL;DR: 在混合专家(MoE)架构中，低资源语言(如希伯来语)存在深层路由崩溃现象，即模型最后几层倾向于将token路由到少数专家子集。通过双语持续预训练可以显著改善这种不平衡，而监督微调效果有限。该现象在多语言环境下普遍存在，且路由改进与下游任务性能提升相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索MoE架构在面对低资源语言(以希伯来语为代表)时，专家路由行为是否存在系统性偏差，以及这种偏差的成因和解决方案。

Method: 分析了两种架构(Qwen3-30B-A3B纯Transformer和Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B混合Mamba-Transformer)在希伯来语上的路由动态，进行双语持续预训练(CPT)和监督微调(SFT)实验，并扩展到日语进行跨语言验证。

Result: 两种预训练模型在低资源语言上均表现出深层路由崩溃(最后几层熵显著下降，token集中在少数专家)；双语CPT能大幅纠正该不平衡，而仅用SFT效果有限；日语结果一致，证明该现象是预训练欠表示的后果而非语言特性；路由改进与下游基准提升相关。

Conclusion: 路由熵和专家专业化程度可作为MoE系统多语言能力的原则性诊断指标，持续预训练是纠正路由偏见的有效手段。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient model scaling, yet expert routing behavior across underrepresented languages remains poorly understood. We analyze routing dynamics in two architecturally distinct MoE models -- a pure Transformer (Qwen3-30B-A3B) and a hybrid Mamba-Transformer (Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B) -- using Hebrew as a morphologically rich, low-resource testbed. Both pre-trained models exhibit \emph{deep-layer routing collapse}: usage entropy drops sharply in final layers and tokens concentrate on a narrow expert subset, a pattern largely absent for English. Continual pre-training (CPT) on balanced bilingual data substantially corrects this imbalance, increasing entropy and shifting routing toward shared, language-agnostic experts; supervised fine-tuning (SFT) alone achieves less complete correction. Extending the analysis to Japanese reveals quantitatively consistent collapse signatures, providing cross-linguistic evidence that the phenomenon is a systematic consequence of pre-training underrepresentation rather than any language-intrinsic property. Routing improvements correlate with consistent downstream benchmark gains, positioning routing entropy and expert specialization as principled diagnostics for multilingual capacity in MoE systems.

</details>


### [295] [Temporal Decay of Co-Citation Predictability: A 20-Year Statute Retrieval Benchmark from 396M Ukrainian Court Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17639)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过构建包含乌克兰101万份判决、3.96亿条引用的大型基准测试UA-StatuteRetrieval，发现法律文献的共引结构随时间显著衰减（Adamic-Adar MRR下降33%-47%），且衰减在不同法域和引用频率层级呈现不均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验法律信息系统中长期以来的一个核心假设：共引结构能提供稳定的检索信号。现有研究缺乏对共引结构随时间变化的大规模、纵向量化分析。

Method: 构建UA-StatuteRetrieval基准，覆盖2007-2026年20个年度快照、101万份乌克兰判决中的3.96亿条法典引用。采用留一法协议分析完整二分引用图，并设置固定文章集和训练/测试时间分割两种评估模式，对比Adamic-Adar、BM25和E5-large嵌入漂移分析。

Result: Adamic-Adar MRR在固定文章集下降33%（0.43→0.29），时间分割下降47%（0.51→0.27）。刑法保持稳定（~0.40），民法从0.35降至0.15（与2017年司法改革吻合）。枢纽文章（>10万引用）抗衰减，中频文章（1K-10K）预测性减半。BM25下降31%，E5-large嵌入显示4.3%语义漂移。

Conclusion: 共引结构的时间衰减是真实存在的现象（非组成偏移或评估伪影），且呈现非均匀模式。法律信息检索系统需引入时间感知机制，尤其关注中频文章的预测性下降问题。

Abstract: Co-citation structure is widely assumed to provide stable retrieval signal in legal information systems. We test this assumption longitudinally by constructing UA-StatuteRetrieval, a benchmark that measures co-citation predictability across 20 annual snapshots (2007-2026) of 396 million codex citations from 101 million Ukrainian court decisions. Using a leave-one-out protocol over the full bipartite citation graph, we find that Adamic-Adar MRR declines 33% on a fixed set of articles (from 0.43 to 0.29) and 47% under a train/test temporal split (from 0.51 to 0.27) confirming genuine temporal decay rather than compositional shift or evaluation artifact. The decay is non-uniform: criminal procedure maintains stable co-citation patterns (MRR ~0.40), while civil law degrades from 0.35 to 0.15, coinciding with the 2017 judicial reform. Hub articles (>100K citations) resist decay, but mid-frequency articles (1K-10K) -- the practical retrieval frontier lose half their predictability. A BM25 text baseline decays even faster (31%), and embedding drift analysis with E5-large reveals a 4.3% semantic shift in how articles are cited, providing a mechanistic explanation for the observed decay. The benchmark is released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/overthelex/ua-statute-retrieval.

</details>


### [296] [Beyond Transcripts: Iterative Peer-Editing with Audio Unlocks High-Quality Human Summaries of Conversational Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17652)
*Kaavya Chaparala,Thomas Thebaud,Jesús Villalba López,Laureano Moro-Velazquez,Peter Viechnicki,Najim Dehak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同人工标注流程（音频、文本或两者结合，以及自我/同行编辑）对语音摘要质量的影响，发现基于音频的摘要信息量较少，但迭代同行编辑可以弥补这一差距，使音频摘要达到与文本摘要和LLM摘要相当的信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音摘要领域缺乏成熟的基准数据集，而创建新基准需要人工标注，因为LLM可能将系统性错误和偏差引入数据集。作者希望探索不同人工标注流程的质量权衡，以建立可靠的数据集收集方法。

Method: 测试了十种标注流程，变量包括输入模态（音频、文本或两者结合）和编辑方式（自我编辑或同行编辑）。将人工基于音频的摘要与基于文本的摘要进行比较，并将人工输出与四个LLM基准（三个文本模型、一个音频模型）进行对比。

Result: 基于音频的摘要信息量较少且更压缩。但迭代同行编辑（结合音频）能缩小这一差距，使音频摘要的信息量与文本摘要和LLM摘要相当。

Conclusion: 迭代同行编辑是人工标注者创建结合词汇和韵律信息的基准的有效方法，即使在无法获得文本转录的场景下也能进行关键的数据集收集。

Abstract: There are not enough established benchmarks for the task fo speech summarization. Creating new benchmarks demands human annotation, as LLMs could embed systemic errors and bias into datasets. We test ten annotation workflows varying input modality (audio, transcript, or both) and the inclusion of editing (self or peer-editing) to investigate potential quality tradeoffs from using human annotators to summarize audio. We compare human audio-based summaries to human transcript-based summaries to track the impact of the different information modalities on summary quality. We also compare the human outputs against four LLM benchmarks (three text, one audio) to examine whether human-written summaries are less informative than highly fluent automated outputs. We find that audio-based summaries are less informative and more compressed than transcript summaries. However, iterative peer-editing with audio mitigates this difference, enabling audio-based summaries to be as informative as their transcript counterparts and LLM summaries. These findings validate iterative peer-editing among human annotators for the creation of benchmarks informed by both lexical and prosodic information. This enables crucial dataset collection even in setting where transcripts are unavailable.

</details>


### [297] [Stop When Reasoning Converges: Semantic-Preserving Early Exit for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17672)
*Dehai Min,Giovanni Vaccarino,Huiyi Chen,Yongliang Wu,Gal Yona,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PUMA框架，通过检测推理级别的语义冗余作为早停信号，结合答案验证，在不牺牲准确率和推理链完整性的前提下，平均减少26.2%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有早停方法仅基于答案级别的信号（如置信度），无法判断推理是否真正收敛，容易过早退出导致推理链语义不完整或准确率下降。

Method: 提出PUMA框架，包含轻量级冗余检测器（检测推理步是否产生新进展）和答案验证模块，前者标记语义冗余的候选退出点，后者确认停止是否安全。

Result: 在5个LRM和5个推理基准上平均减少26.2% token，保持准确率和CoT质量；在代码生成、零样本视觉-语言推理等任务上也有良好泛化。

Conclusion: 推理级别的语义冗余是一种鲁棒、可迁移、可学习的信号，能够有效指导高效推理的早停策略。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance by generating long chains of thought (CoT), but often overthink, continuing to reason after a solution has already stabilized and thereby wasting tokens and increasing latency. Existing inference-time early-exit methods rely primarily on answer-level signals, such as confidence or trial-answer consistency, to decide when to stop. However, these signals mainly reflect answer readiness rather than reasoning convergence: they may trigger before the model has finished exploring or self-correcting, causing premature exits that can degrade final-answer accuracy and leave the retained reasoning chain semantically incomplete. We identify reasoning-level semantic redundancy as a complementary signal for semantic-preserving early exit: when successive steps no longer add novel progress and instead revisit established conclusions, the reasoning trajectory has likely converged. Building on this insight, we propose PUMA, a plug-and-play framework that combines a lightweight Redundancy Detector with answer-level verification. The detector flags semantically redundant candidate exits, while verification confirms whether stopping is safe, allowing PUMA to remove redundant continuation while preserving both answer accuracy and a coherent reasoning prefix. Across five LRMs and five challenging reasoning benchmarks, PUMA achieves 26.2% average token reduction while preserving accuracy and retained CoT quality. Additional experiments on code generation, zero-shot vision-language reasoning, and learned stopping-policy internalization further demonstrate that reasoning-level redundancy is a robust, transferable, and learnable signal for efficient reasoning. Our code is available at \url{https://github.com/giovanni-vaccarino/PUMA}.

</details>


### [298] [Validate Your Authority: Benchmarking LLMs on Multi-Label Precedent Treatment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17691)
*M. Mikail Demir,M. Abdullah Canbaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对法律先例中负面处理分类任务，提出了新的评估框架、专家标注数据集和平均严重性误差指标，并对比了现代大语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率在法律文本分类中存在不足，误分类风险高，亟需更鲁棒的评估方法。

Method: 构建了239条真实法律引用的专家标注数据集，提出平均严重性误差（Average Severity Error）指标，并评测了多个现代大语言模型。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash在高层分类任务中准确率最高（79.1%）；GPT-5-mini在细粒度分类任务中表现最佳（67.7%）。

Conclusion: 本研究为复杂法律推理任务建立了基线，提供了新数据集和针对性的评估指标。

Abstract: Automating the classification of negative treatment in legal precedent is a critical yet nuanced NLP task where misclassification carries significant risk. To address the shortcomings of standard accuracy, this paper introduces a more robust evaluation framework. We benchmark modern Large Language Models on a new, expert-annotated dataset of 239 real-world legal citations and propose a novel Average Severity Error metric to better measure the practical impact of classification errors. Our experiments reveal a performance split. Google's Gemini 2.5 Flash achieved the highest accuracy on a high-level classification task (79.1%), while OpenAI's GPT-5-mini was the top performer on the more complex fine-grained schema (67.7%). This work establishes a crucial baseline, provides a new context-rich dataset, and introduces an evaluation metric tailored to the demands of this complex legal reasoning task.

</details>


### [299] [Do LLM Agents Mirror Socio-Cognitive Effects in Power-Asymmetric Conversations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17694)
*Anvesh Rao Vijjini,Sagar Manjunath,Snigdha Chaturvedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过为大型语言模型（LLMs）分配高低权力身份角色，模拟多轮权力不对称对话（如校长-教师、法官-律师），测量语言协调、代词使用、说服成功率和服从不安全请求等指标，发现LLMs表现出与人类类似的权力社会认知效应，但存在细微差异和变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力差异通过社会认知效应影响人类沟通，包括语言协调、代词使用、权威偏见和有害服从等。研究者想探究LLM在分配高低权力身份时是否也会表现出类似的人类行为模式。

Method: 使用来自不同职业的身份角色（如校长-教师、法官-律师），模拟多轮权力不对称对话，测量四个维度：(i)语言协调程度，(ii)代词使用模式，(iii)说服成功率，(iv)对不安全请求的服从程度。

Result: LLMs展示了关键的社会认知权力效应，但存在细微差异和变异性，模拟互动既关联到理想行为也关联到不安全行为。

Conclusion: LLMs在权力不对称对话中会复现人类的社会认知权力效应，这既可能产生有益结果（如更好的协调），也可能带来风险（如对不安全请求的服从）。

Abstract: Power differences shape human communication through well documented socio cognitive effects, including language coordination, pronoun usage, authority bias, and harmful compliance. We examine whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit similar behaviors when assigned high or low status personas. Using personas from diverse professions, we simulate multi turn, power asymmetric dialogues (e.g., principal teacher, justice lawyer) and measure (i) linguistic coordination, (ii) pronoun usage, (iii) persuasion success, and (iv) compliance with unsafe requests. Our results show that LLMs show key socio cognitive effects of power, albeit with nuances and variability, linking simulated interactions to both desirable and unsafe behaviors.

</details>


### [300] [Sometin Beta Pass Notin (SBPN): Improving Multilingual ASR for Nigerian Languages via Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17710)
*Sewade Ogun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段蒸馏+自改进的多语言ASR框架SBPN，覆盖5种尼日利亚语言，相比单语基线平均WER降低29%，超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼日利亚语言在ASR系统中性能远低于英语、法语等高资源语言，面临数据稀缺、正字法不一致、声调符号、口音多样、频繁语码切换及本地化实体等独特建模挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段蒸馏：先通过学生-教师知识蒸馏从现有单语模型出发，结合语言特定的N-gram语言模型；再通过伪标签数据进行迭代自改进来进一步提升准确率。

Result: 相比单语基线平均相对WER降低29%，在Common Voice和Fleurs等主流基准上超越现有最先进的多语言模型。

Conclusion: 发布开源多语言ASR基础模型SBPN（Base 120M和Large 600M参数），覆盖约鲁巴语、豪萨语、伊博语、尼日利亚皮钦语和尼日利亚英语，为区域语音研究提供资源。

Abstract: Although modern multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems support several Nigerian languages, their performance consistently lags behind high-resource languages like English and French. Nigerian languages present unique modelling hurdles, including acute data scarcity, inconsistent orthography, tonal diacritics, diverse accents, frequent code-switching, and localized named entities. To address these challenges, we developed a multilingual ASR framework utilizing a two-stage distillation process. First, we employ student-teacher knowledge distillation from existing monolingual models, conditioned on robust language-specific N-gram language models. Second, we perform iterative self improvement using pseudo-labelled data to further refine accuracy. Our method significantly bridges the performance gap, achieving on average a relative Word Error Rate (WER) reduction of 29 % over monolingual baselines. Our models also outperform state-of-the-art multilingual models across major benchmarks, including Common Voice and Fleurs. We introduce Sometin Beta Pass Notin (SBPN), a foundational multilingual ASR model covering Yorùbá, Hausa, Igbo, Nigerian Pidgin, and Nigerian English. SBPN is released in two sizes: SBPN-Base (120 M parameters) and SBPN-Large (600 M parameters). By releasing these as open foundation models, we aim to provide ASR resources for further research into the rich phonetic and cultural landscape of the region.

</details>


### [301] [Bridging the Version Gap: Multi-version Training Improves ICD Code Prediction, Especially for Rare Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17755)
*Jinghui Liu,Anthony Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了结合不同版本ICD编码数据训练版本无关的临床编码模型，发现加入ICD-9数据能使ICD-10预测中罕见代码的micro F1提升27%，同时改善高频代码的宏观指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICD编码系统不断演变且各地区版本不同，现有模型通常针对特定ICD版本优化，且面临长尾问题（罕见代码性能差），因此希望探索是否可以通过合并不同ICD版本的数据训练版本无关模型来解决这些挑战。

Method: 采用改进的标签式注意力模型，将ICD-9标注数据加入ICD-10预测任务的训练中，进行多版本联合训练。

Result: 加入ICD-9数据后，18,000个罕见ICD-10代码的micro F1提升27%；8,000个高频ICD-10代码的宏观指标显著改善，且模型参数量更少。

Conclusion: 跨版本ICD数据融合是可行的，能有效缓解长尾问题并提升模型性能，为实际临床编码自动化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Clinical coding maps clinical documentation to standardized medical codes, an essential yet time-consuming administrative task that could benefit from automation. Current models on ICD coding are typically optimized for codes from a specific ICD version. However, in reality, ICD systems evolve continuously, and different versions are adopted across time periods and regions. Moreover, ICD coding suffers from the long-tail problem, and rare code performance can be a bottleneck for developing implementable models. We examine whether it is viable to train version-independent models by combining data annotated in different ICD versions, which may help address these challenges. We add ICD-9 data to the training of a modified label-wise attention model for ICD-10 prediction, and find that despite the version mismatch, adding ICD-9 yields a 27% increase in micro F1 for 18K rare ICD codes compared to training on ICD-10 alone. On 8K frequent ICD-10 codes, the multi-version training also substantially improves macro metrics, with far fewer model parameters.

</details>


### [302] [Internalizing Tool Knowledge in Small Language Models via QLoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17774)
*Yuval Shemla,Ayal Yakobe,Tanmay Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出通过QLoRA微调小语言模型，将工具使用知识内化到模型权重中，从而在推理时无需提供完整工具描述，显著减少token开销（输入长度减少82.6%），并在结构化规划评分上超越有完整工具描述的未微调基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用流水线需要在每次推理时包含完整工具模式，造成大量token开销，限制了小模型的实际应用。本文探索是否可以通过参数高效微调将工具使用知识内化到小语言模型中，实现无需工具描述的推理。

Method: 使用AssetOpsBench作为基准，采用8位QLoRA对Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3-4B进行微调，训练数据约1700条工具使用样本，涵盖工具知识、问题到规划的映射及执行轨迹。推理时采用无描述推理，即提示中完全省略工具目录。

Result: 微调模型在输入长度减少82.6%的情况下，结构化规划和LLM评判规划得分均优于有完整工具描述的未微调基线。最佳Gemma模型的AT-F1为0.65，总体评判得分为3.88（基线为0.47和2.88）。Qwen3-4B以更少内存和更快速度获得3.78的总体评判得分，但在通用多项选择基准上表现出更严重的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 对于固定工具目录，QLoRA微调可以将工具知识从提示上下文转移到模型权重中，在保持或提升工具规划质量的同时，大幅降低推理开销。LoRA秩控制质量-保留权衡，r=32最大化规划质量，较小秩则更好保留通用知识。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as planning components in agentic systems, but current tool-use pipelines often require full tool schemas to be included in every prompt, creating substantial token overhead and limiting the practicality of smaller models. This paper investigates whether tool-use knowledge can be internalized into small language models through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, enabling structured planning without explicit tool descriptions at inference time. Using AssetOpsBench as the primary benchmark, we fine-tune Gemma 4 E4B and Qwen3-4B with 8-bit QLoRA on approximately 1,700 tool-use examples spanning tool knowledge, question-to-plan mappings, and execution-style traces. We evaluate the resulting models under description-free inference, where the prompt omits the tool catalog entirely. The fine-tuned models outperform an informed unfine-tuned baseline that receives full tool descriptions, reducing input length by 82.6\% while improving structural and LLM-judge planning scores. In the best Gemma run, the model achieves an AT-F1 of 0.65 and an overall judge score of 3.88, compared with 0.47 and 2.88 for the informed baseline. Qwen3-4B achieves a strong overall judge score of 3.78 while using 62\% less memory and running 2.5$\times$ faster than Gemma, though it also exhibits greater catastrophic forgetting on general multiple-choice benchmarks. Additional ablations show that LoRA rank controls a quality--retention trade-off, with $r=32$ maximizing planning quality and smaller ranks preserving more general knowledge. These results suggest that, for fixed tool catalogs, QLoRA fine-tuning can shift tool knowledge from prompt context into model weights, substantially reducing inference overhead while maintaining or improving tool-planning quality.

</details>


### [303] [Systematic Evaluation of the Quality of Synthetic Clinical Notes Rephrased by LLMs at Million-Note Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17775)
*Jinghui Liu,Sarvesh Soni,Anthony Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM生成的临床文本，发现在保留核心信息和粗粒度任务预测能力的同时，会丢失细粒度细节（如ICD编码），并分析了错误类型及通过分块重写、数据增强等方法缓解问题的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM生成临床文本的评估往往只关注单一维度（如相似性或实用性），缺乏系统性、多维度的综合评价，因此需要从内在、外在和事实性三个维度进行全面评估。

Method: 基于MIMIC数据库以百万级规模重写临床笔记，进行内在评估（语言质量）、外在评估（预测任务效用）和事实性评估（事实核查与错误分析），并比较全篇重写与分块重写的效果差异。

Result: 合成笔记保留了核心临床信息和粗粒度任务预测能力，但丢失了ICD编码等细粒度细节；分块重写可缓解细节丢失但降低事实精度；错误以临床语境误解为主，包含时间混淆、测量错误和捏造信息；合成笔记可有效增强罕见ICD编码的任务训练。

Conclusion: LLM生成的临床文本在粗粒度任务上表现良好，但细粒度任务需要进一步优化；分块重写是一种折中方案；合成数据对低资源场景（如罕见ICD编码）具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate or synthesize clinical text for a wide range of applications, from improving clinical documentation to augmenting clinical text analytics. Yet evaluations typically focus on a narrow aspect -- such as similarity or utility comparisons -- even though these aspects are complementary and best viewed in parallel. In this study, we aim to conduct a systematic evaluation of LLM-generated clinical text, which includes intrinsic, extrinsic, and factuality evaluations of synthetic clinical notes rephrased from MIMIC databases at million-note scale. Our analysis demonstrates that synthetic notes preserve core clinical information and predictive utility for coarse-grained tasks despite substantial linguistic changes, but lose fine-grained details for task like ICD coding. We show this loss of detail can be substantially mitigated by rephrasing notes by chunks rather than by the whole note, but at the cost of reduced factual precision under incomplete context. Through fact-checking and error analysis, we further find that synthesis errors are dominated by misinterpretation of clinical context, alongside temporal confusion, measurement errors, and fabricated claims. Finally, we show that the synthetic notes -- despite their task-agnostic nature -- can effectively augment task-specific training for rare ICD codes.

</details>


### [304] [Generating Pretraining Tokens from Organic Data for Data-Bound Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17849)
*Zichun Yu,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynPro是一个合成数据生成框架，通过改写和重新格式化操作，帮助LLM在数据受限的情况下更充分地利用有限的有机数据，在400M和1.1B模型上实现3.7-5.2倍的有效token提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练正从计算受限转向数据受限，可用的有机文本远不能满足规模化需求，但模型并未充分学习已有有机语料。

Method: SynPro包含两个操作：改写(Rephrasing)和重新格式化(Reformat)，通过强化学习优化生成器，奖励包括质量、忠实度和数据影响力，并在预训练平台期持续更新以针对模型未吸收的内容。

Result: 在400M和1.1B模型上，使用10%的Chinchilla最优token，SynPro解锁了3.7-5.2倍的有效token，在1.1B规模上甚至超越了非数据受限的oracle基准。

Conclusion: 有机数据被标准重复方式显著低估，忠实且模型感知的合成数据生成可以持续支撑数据受限的规模化，且不会导致分布崩塌。

Abstract: LLM pretraining is shifting from a compute-bound to a data-bound regime, where available human (organic) text falls far short of scaling demands. However, reaching the data-bound regime does not mean the model has fully utilized its organic corpus. In this paper, we introduce SynPro, a synthetic data generation framework that helps LLMs more thoroughly learn from limited organic data. SynPro applies two operations, rephrasing and reformat, that present the same organic source in diverse forms to facilitate deeper learning without introducing external information. Both generators are optimized via reinforcement learning with quality, faithfulness, and data influence rewards, and are continuously updated as pretraining plateaus to target content the model has yet to absorb. We pretrain 400M and 1.1B models with 10% of their Chinchilla-optimal tokens (0.8B and 2.2B) from DCLM-Baseline, reflecting a realistic data-bound regime in frontier pretraining. Our results reveal that organic data is significantly underutilized by standard repetition: SynPro unlocks 3.7-5.2x the effective tokens of repetition, even surpassing the non-data-bound oracle that trains on equivalent unique data at the 1.1B scale. Analyses confirm that faithful, model-aware synthesis sustains data-bound scaling without causing distribution collapse. We open-source our code at https://github.com/cxcscmu/SynPro.

</details>


### [305] [PAREDA: A Multi-Accent Speech Dataset of Natural Language Processing Research Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17860)
*Sicheng Jin,Dipankar Srirag,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAREDA数据集——首个包含澳大利亚、印度英语和中国英语三种口音、针对NLP学术论文讨论的多口音语音数据集，涵盖自发性独白和问答对话。实验表明，SOTA ASR模型在该数据集零样本场景下性能下降，但微调后显著降低了词错误率，验证了该数据集对构建鲁棒ASR系统的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统在基准测试中表现优异，但在真实场景下（尤其是面对带口音、自发性和领域特异性语音）性能显著下降，缺乏能反映这些挑战的多口音专业领域语音数据集。

Method: 构建了PAREDA数据集，包含澳大利亚、印度英语和中国英语三种口音，以NLP学术论文为讨论主题，采集了自发性独白（论文摘要总结）和非独白（问答对话）两种语音类型；随后评估SOTA ASR模型在零样本和微调设置下的表现。

Result: 零样本设置下ASR模型表现较差，证实了数据集的挑战性；在PAREDA上微调后，词错误率显著降低。

Conclusion: PAREDA数据集捕捉了现有语料库常缺失的语言特征，是构建和评估面向专业化真实场景的鲁棒、包容性ASR系统的宝贵资源。

Abstract: While modern Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems achieve high accuracy on benchmark corpora, their performance often degrades when there is real-world variability. This work focuses on variability arising due to accented, spontaneous, and domain-specific speech. In particular, we introduce PAper REading DAtaset (PAREDA), a first-of-its-kind multi-accent speech dataset consisting of discussions on academic Natural Language Processing (NLP) papers between speakers with Australian, Indian-English, and Chinese English accents. Each session elicits a spontaneous monologue (a summary of a paper's abstract) and a non-monologue (a question-and-answer session between participants), resulting in a corpus rich with technical jargon and conversational phenomena. We evaluate the performance of SOTA ASR models on PAREDA, analysing the impact of accent mixing and increased speech rate. Our results show that, in the zero-shot setting, models perform worse, confirming the dataset's challenging nature. However, fine-tuning on PAREDA significantly reduces the Word Error Rate (WER), demonstrating that our dataset captures linguistic characteristics often missing from existing corpora. PAREDA serves as a valuable new resource for building and evaluating more robust and inclusive ASR systems for specialised, real-world applications.

</details>


### [306] [Multi-agent AI systems outperform human teams in creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17885)
*Tiancheng Hu,Yixuan Jiang,Haotian Li,José Hernández-Orallo,Xing Xie,Nigel Collier,David Stillwell,Luning Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，基于大语言模型的多智能体团队在创造力上显著超越人类团队（Cohen's d=1.50），优势主要来自新颖性，而实用性相当。通过语义空间分析发现，两者在对话模式上存在差异：LLM团队受益于高效探索（高语义扩展、短路径），人类团队则依赖流畅对话（高局部连贯性、频繁转折）。模型选择和讨论结构可解释26.8%的对话动态差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI已在许多认知任务上达到甚至超越人类水平，但创造力仍是一个备受争议的前沿领域。随着基于LLM的AI系统在研究创新中日益普及，理解和增强其创造力至关重要。

Method: 通过对比4,541个多智能体LLM创意与341个人类团队创意，在六个问题解决任务上评估创造力。使用神经语言模型表示将对话映射为语义空间中的路径，分析全局连贯性、局部连贯性、语义扩展和转折频率等特征。

Result: 多智能体LLM团队在创造力上显著超越人类团队（Cohen's d=1.50），优势主要由新颖性驱动，实用性相当。两类团队在低全局连贯性时创造力更强，但LLM团队受益于高效探索（高语义扩展、短路径），人类团队受益于流畅对话（高局部连贯性、频繁转折）。模型选择和讨论结构可解释26.8%的对话动态差异。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统在创造力任务上不仅超越单智能体，也大幅超越人类团队，为开发增强创造力的多智能体系统提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: Although artificial intelligence (AI) now matches or exceeds human performance across numerous cognitive tasks, creativity remains a highly contested frontier. As AI systems based on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted in research and innovation, it is essential to understand and augment their creativity. Here we demonstrate that multi-agent LLM teams not only surpass single agents, but also substantially outperform human teams in creativity (Cohen's d=1.50) across 4,541 multi-agent LLM ideas and 341 human-team ideas on six diverse problem-solving tasks. This advantage is driven by novelty while maintaining comparable usefulness. To investigate the generative processes in both groups, we represent conversations as paths through semantic space using neural language model representations. Both LLM and human teams produce more creative ideas when conversations range widely rather than staying centered on a single theme (low global coherence). However, the additional patterns that predict creativity differ: LLM teams benefit from efficient exploration (high semantic spread, shorter paths), while human teams benefit from maintaining smooth conversational flow (high local coherence, frequent pivots). Additionally, we identify model choice and discussion structure as orthogonal design levers that together explain 26.8% of variance in LLM conversational dynamics, paving the way for systematic approaches to developing multi-agent systems with augmented creative capabilities.

</details>


### [307] [A Pilot Benchmark for NL-to-FOL Translation in Planetary Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17911)
*Hayden Moore,Suman Saha,Mahfuza Farooque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将行星探索任务自然语言文档转化为一阶逻辑表示的基准数据集，基于NASA真实任务文档构建，旨在支撑语言理解与形式推理的交叉研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星探测中，自主机器人需在通信受限、无GPS环境下推理任务目标与约束，但现有研究多聚焦感知与控制，高层面任务知识转化为机器可解释表示的工作尚不充分。

Method: 基于NASA行星数据系统2003-2013年真实任务文档，手动注释自然语言文本对应的一阶逻辑表示，涵盖时间结构、智能体角色和操作依赖，并提供结构化的谓词词汇表和类型化常量。

Result: 构建了一个用于自然语言到一阶逻辑翻译的初步基准数据集，为面向安全关键性任务的语言理解与形式推理研究提供基础。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了任务知识形式化表示的空白，为未来在真实任务场景中结合语言理解与逻辑推理的研究提供了实验基础和数据支撑。

Abstract: Future planetary exploration envisions autonomous robotic agents operating under severe communication constraints, without global positioning, and with minimal human intervention. In such environments, agents must not only perceive and act, but also reason over mission objectives, operational constraints, and evolving environmental conditions. While prior work has largely focused on perception and control, the translation of high-level mission knowledge into structured, machine-interpretable representations remains underexplored.
  We introduce a pilot benchmark for translating natural language (NL) into First-Order Logic (FOL) within the domain of planetary exploration. The dataset is constructed from real mission documentation sourced from NASA's Planetary Data System (PDS), spanning missions from 2003 to 2013. These documents describe mission phases such as launch, boost, coast, cruise, and orbital operations in rich natural language. We manually annotate these documents with corresponding FOL representations that capture temporal structure, agent roles, and operational dependencies. In addition, we provide structured predicate vocabularies and typed constants to enable controlled experimentation with varying levels of prior knowledge. This pilot benchmark provides a foundation for research at the intersection of language understanding and formal reasoning, grounded in real-world, safety-critical mission data. The dataset is provided at: https://github.com/HaydenMM/planetary-logic-benchmark/blob/main/pilot_benchmark.json

</details>


### [308] [Universal Adversarial Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17936)
*Benedict Florance Arockiaraj,Alexander Feng,Jianxiong Cai,Xiaoyu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合词性过滤和困惑度损失函数的方法，生成更自然、更符合语法的对抗触发词，用于通用对抗攻击。在SST情感分析任务上，该方法能生成有效触发词将准确率降至0.04和0.12，并通过对抗训练将模型鲁棒性从0.12提升至0.48。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用对抗攻击生成的触发词不自然、不合语法，容易被检测，因此需要生成更接近自然短语的触发词以提高攻击隐蔽性，并促进模型鲁棒性研究。

Method: 结合词性过滤（Parts-of-Speech Filtering）和基于困惑度（Perplexity）的损失函数，从自然语言中筛选更合理的触发序列。

Result: 在SST数据集上，生成的触发词可将正面转负面预测的准确率降至0.04，负面转正面降至0.12；对抗训练后模型准确率从0.12提升至0.48。

Conclusion: 对抗攻击可以通过生成更自然的触发词变得难以检测，同时对抗训练能有效提升模型鲁棒性，表明兼顾攻击隐蔽性与防御训练的重要性。

Abstract: Recent works have illustrated that modern NLP models trained for diverse tasks ranging from sentiment analysis to language generation succumb to universal adversarial attacks, a class of input-agnostic attacks where a common trigger sequence is used to attack the model. Although these attacks are successful, the triggers generated by such attacks are ungrammatical and unnatural. Our work proposes a novel technique combining parts-of-speech filtering and perplexity based loss function to generate sensible triggers that are closer to natural phrases. For the task of sentiment analysis on the SST dataset, the method produces sensible triggers that achieve accuracies as low as 0.04 and 0.12 for flipping positive to negative predictions and vice-versa. To build robust models, we also perform adversarial training using the generated triggers that increases the accuracy of the model from 0.12 to 0.48. We aim to illustrate that adversarial attacks can be made difficult to detect by generating sensible triggers, and to facilitate robust model development through relevant defenses.

</details>


### [309] [BacktestBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Automated Quantitative Strategy Backtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17937)
*Zhensheng Wang,Wenmian Yang,Qingtai Wu,Lequan Ma,Yiquan Zhang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BacktestBench——首个大规模自动化量化回测基准，包含18,246个问答对，并设计了多智能体基线系统AutoBacktest，在23个主流大语言模型上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化回测评估交易策略至关重要，但存在技术门槛高、可扩展性差的问题。尽管大语言模型有望通过代码生成、工具使用和智能规划来自动化这一流程，但缺乏大规模专用基准数据集严重阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了基于600万条真实市场数据的BacktestBench基准（18,246个问答对，涵盖指标计算、代码选择、策略选择、参数确认四类任务），并提出AutoBacktest多智能体基线系统，包含Summarizer（语义因子提取）、Retriever（SQL生成验证）、Coder（Python回测实现）三个协作模块。

Result: 在23个主流大语言模型上进行了评估，通过消融实验识别了影响端到端性能的关键因素，强调了基于事实的验证和标准化指标表示的重要性。

Conclusion: BacktestBench填补了自动化量化回测领域缺乏大规模基准的空白，AutoBacktest为将自然语言策略转化为可复现回测提供了有效基线，为未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantitative backtesting is essential for evaluating trading strategies but remains hampered by high technical barriers and limited scalability. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a transformative path to automate this complex, interdisciplinary workflow through advanced code generation, tool usage, and agentic planning, the practical realization is significantly challenged by the current lack of a large-scale benchmark dedicated to automated quantitative backtesting, which hinders progress in this field. To bridge this critical gap, we introduce BacktestBench, the first large-scale benchmark for automated quantitative backtesting. Built from over 6 million real market records, it comprises 18,246 meticulously annotated question-answering pairs across four task categories: metrics calculation, ticker selection, strategy selection, and parameter confirmation. We also propose AutoBacktest, a robust multi-agent baseline that translates natural language strategies into reproducible backtests by coordinating a Summarizer for semantic factor extraction, a Retriever for validated SQL generation, and a Coder for Python backtesting implementation. Our evaluation on 23 mainstream LLMs, complemented by targeted ablations, identifies key factors that influence end-to-end performance and highlights the importance of grounded verification and standardized indicator representations.

</details>


### [310] [AutoVecCoder: Teaching LLMs to Generate Explicitly Vectorized Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17978)
*Shangzhan Li,Xinyu Yin,Xuanyu Jin,Ye He,Yuxin Zhou,Yuxuan Li,Xu Han,Wanxiang Che,Qi Shi,Ting Liu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoVecCoder框架，通过VecPrompt数据合成和VecRL强化学习，使大语言模型具备自动显式向量化能力，在SIMD基准测试上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器自动向量化由于保守的静态分析常产生次优结果，而开发者使用内联函数进行显式向量化门槛高。大语言模型虽擅长通用代码生成，但因高质量语料稀缺及底层硬件指令的严格语义约束，难以胜任显式向量化任务。

Method: 提出AutoVecCoder框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) VecPrompt——自动化数据合成流水线，注入领域特定的内联函数知识；(2) VecRL——强化学习框架，将代码生成与执行效率对齐，优化生成代码的实际性能。

Result: AutoVecCoder-8B在SimdBench的SSE和AVX子集上达到最先进性能，部分情况下生成的实现超越了标准-O3优化级别。

Conclusion: AutoVecCoder有效克服了传统自动向量化的固有瓶颈，证明了通过数据合成与强化学习结合可以显著提升大语言模型在显式向量化任务上的能力。

Abstract: Vectorization via Single Instruction, Multiple Data (SIMD) architectures is a cornerstone of high-performance computing. To fully exploit hardware potential, developers often resort to explicit vectorization using intrinsics, as compiler-based auto-vectorization frequently yields suboptimal results due to conservative static analysis. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in general code generation, they struggle with explicit vectorization due to the scarcity of high-quality corpora and the strict semantic constraints of low-level hardware instructions. In this paper, we propose AutoVecCoder, a novel framework designed to empower LLMs with the capability of automated explicit vectorization. AutoVecCoder integrates two core components: VecPrompt, an automated data synthesis pipeline to inject domain-specific intrinsic knowledge; and VecRL, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns code generation with execution efficiency. AutoVecCoder-8B trained by this framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the SSE and AVX subsets of SimdBench and, in some cases, generates implementations surpassing standard -O3 optimizations, effectively overcoming the inherent bottlenecks of traditional automated vectorization.

</details>


### [311] [Predictive Prefetching for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17989)
*Wuyang Zhang,Shichao Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种预测性预取异步检索框架，通过语义前兆预测检索时机与内容，在保持答案质量的同时显著降低RAG系统延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步RAG存在较大延迟，现有异步方法依赖启发式协调且假设信息需求稳定，复杂多领域场景下效果不佳。

Method: 提出包含三个组件（检索预测器、上下文监视器、查询生成器）的异步检索框架，利用生成动态中的语义前兆在不确定性达到临界点之前预取信息。

Result: 端到端延迟降低高达43.5%，首个令牌生成时间提升62.4%，答案质量与同步RAG基线相当。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了异步RAG中检索与生成的协调问题，实现了更高效、自适应的检索增强生成。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves factual grounding in large language models but suffers from substantial latency due to synchronous retrieval. While recent work explores asynchronous retrieval, existing approaches rely on heuristic coordination between retrieval and generation and assume stable information demands during decoding that often break in complex, multi-domain settings. In this paper, we propose an advanced asynchronous retrieval framework that enables predictive prefetching aligned with evolving information needs. The framework explicitly predicts when retrieval should be triggered and what information should be retrieved using three components, a retrieval predictor, a context monitor, and a query generator, by exploiting semantic precursors in generation dynamics that emerge several tokens before uncertainty becomes critical. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate up to 43.5% end-to-end latency reduction and 62.4% improvement in time-to-first-token, while maintaining answer quality comparable to synchronous RAG baselines.

</details>


### [312] [Semantic Reranking at Inference Time for Hard Examples in Rhetorical Role Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18007)
*Anas Belfathi,Nicolas Hernandez,Laura Monceaux,Warren Bonnard,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RISE是一个推理阶段的语义重排序框架，利用标签语义信息来改进Rhetorical Role Labeling任务中困难样本的预测效果，在8个领域数据集上平均提升9.15个macro-F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在RRL任务中对困难样本预测不可靠，而现有方法通常隐式处理不确定性，且将标签视为离散标识符，忽略了标签名称中蕴含的语义信息。

Method: 提出RISE框架，自动识别低置信度预测，利用对比学习得到的标签表示对模型输出进行重排序，无需重新训练或修改原模型。

Result: 在8个领域特定RRL数据集上使用7种语言模型（包括编码器架构和因果架构），困难样本平均提升+9.15 macro-F1点。人工难度标注与模型难度之间Cohen's kappa=0.40，呈现中等一致性。

Conclusion: RISE能有效提升RRL任务中困难样本的预测可靠性，且标签语义信息对模型性能有重要价值。

Abstract: Rhetorical Role Labeling (RRL) assigns a functional role to each sentence in a document and is widely used in legal, medical, and scientific domains. While language models (LMs) achieve strong average performance, they remain unreliable on hard examples, where prediction confidence is low. Existing approaches typically handle uncertainty implicitly and treat labels as discrete identifiers, overlooking the semantic information encoded in label names. We introduce RISE, an inference-time semantic reranking framework that leverages label semantics to refine predictions on hard instances. RISE automatically identifies low-confidence predictions and reranks model outputs using contrastively learned label representations, without retraining or modifying the underlying model. Experiments on eight domain-specific RRL datasets with seven LMs, including encoder-based and causal architectures, show an average gain of +9.15 macro-F1 points on hard examples. For explainability, we further propose manual hardness annotations to study difficulty from both model and human perspectives, revealing a moderate agreement with Cohen's kappa = 0.40.

</details>


### [313] [PROTEA: Offline Evaluation and Iterative Refinement for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18032)
*Kazuki Kawamura,Satoshi Waki,Kei Tateno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PROTEA是一个用于多智能体LLM工作流的离线测试驱动改进的统一界面，通过节点评分、反向评估和可编辑提示修订，帮助开发者定位和修复工作流中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM工作流虽优于单提示基线，但调试和优化困难，错误源于中间输出的细微问题并传播到下游节点，开发者需检查长追踪并推断修改哪个智能体。

Method: PROTEA执行工作流，用可配置评分标准对中间节点输出评分，在工作流图上叠加节点状态和推理信息以定位瓶颈；支持从最终答案引用反向生成节点级预期，与观察输出比较；对选中节点展示前后编辑对比的提示修订，自动重新运行和评估。

Result: 在两个生产相关工作流中，文档检查准确率从64.3%提升至83.9%，推荐Hit@5从0.30提升至0.38；六位资深LLM开发者的形成性研究中，参与者重视图级定位、节点推理和可编辑的前后提示修订。

Conclusion: PROTEA通过图级瓶颈定位、反向节点评估和迭代提示修订，有效提升了多智能体工作流的调试效率和性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM workflows -- systems composed of multiple role-specific LLM calls -- often outperform single-prompt baselines, but they remain difficult to debug and refine. Failures can originate from subtle errors in intermediate outputs that propagate to downstream nodes, requiring developers to inspect long traces and infer which agent to modify. We present PROTEA, a unified interface for offline, test-driven improvement of multi-agent workflows. PROTEA executes a workflow, scores intermediate node outputs with configurable rubrics, and overlays per-node states and rationales on the workflow graph to localize likely bottlenecks. To support complex systems where final-answer references are the primary supervision, PROTEA performs backward node evaluation: it generates candidate node-level expectations from final-answer references and graph context, then compares them with observed node outputs. For selected nodes, PROTEA presents targeted prompt revisions as editable before/after comparisons, then automatically reruns and re-evaluates the workflow to show output changes and score trajectories within the same interface. In two production-adjacent workflows, PROTEA improved document-inspection accuracy from 64.3% to 83.9% and recommendation Hit@5 from 0.30 to 0.38. In a formative study with six experienced LLM developers, participants valued graph-level localization, per-node rationales, and editable before/after prompt revisions.

</details>


### [314] [KVDrive: A Holistic Multi-Tier KV Cache Management System for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18071)
*Jian Lin,Jiazhi Mi,Zicong Hong,Haodong Wang,Qianli Liu,Haodyue Zhang,Peng Li,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KVDrive是一个多层级KV缓存管理系统，通过协调GPU内存、主机DRAM和SSD之间的缓存放置、流水线调度和跨层协同，有效解决了长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存传输成为解码延迟主要瓶颈的问题，相比现有方案吞吐量提升高达1.74倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存卸载系统在长上下文和大批量场景下，KV传输量急剧增加成为解码延迟的主要来源，且通过算法提升稀疏性已接近天花板，难以在保证精度的同时进一步降低传输开销。

Method: KVDrive提出一种整体性的多层级KV缓存管理系统：1) 根据注意力行为自适应管理缓存，最大化重用并减少冗余数据移动；2) 重构解码流水线，使I/O与CPU/GPU计算阶段重叠，消除异构资源间的停顿；3) 协调跨内存层级的数据移动，突破GPU和DRAM的容量限制。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试中，使用主流LLM评估，KVDrive相比现有最优方案实现了最高1.74倍的吞吐量提升，同时保持了模型的准确性。

Conclusion: 从系统层面而非仅算法层面解决KV缓存管理问题，通过多层级协调和流水线优化可以有效提升长上下文LLM推理效率，为未来更大规模推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Supporting long-context LLMs is challenging due to the substantial memory demands of the key-value (KV) cache. Existing offloading systems store the full cache in host memory and selectively fetch critical entries during decoding, but this strategy quickly hits a ceiling: sparsity cannot be pushed further without degrading accuracy. As a result, when context length and batch size grow, the volume of KV transfers rises sharply and becomes the dominant source of decoding latency. We present KVDrive, a holistic multi-tier KV cache management system spanning GPU memory, host DRAM, and SSD. Unlike prior work that pursues greater sparsity through algorithmic refinements, KVDrive tackles the problem from a systems perspective - jointly orchestrating cache placement, pipeline scheduling, and cross-tier coordination to sustain high-throughput inference under tight GPU budgets. KVDrive advances three fundamental capabilities: it adapts cache management to attention behavior to maximize reuse and minimize redundant data movement; it restructures the decoding pipeline to overlap I/O- and CPU/GPU compute-bound stages, eliminating stalls across heterogeneous resources; and it harmonizes data movement across memory tiers to unlock scalable long-context inference far beyond GPU and DRAM limits. We have implemented a fully functional prototype of KVDrive and evaluated it on long-context benchmarks with popular LLMs. The system achieves up to 1.74x higher throughput compared to state-of-the-art works while preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [315] [A Data-Efficient Path to Multilingual LLMs: Language Expansion via Post-training PARAM$Δ$ Integration into Upcycled MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18083)
*Hao Zhou,Tianhao Li,Zhijun Wang,Shuaijie She,Linjuan Wu,Hao-Ran Wei,Baosong Yang,Jiajun Chen,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出METHOD方法，通过将稠密模型升级为MoE架构为不同语言分配不同专家，并移植MoE扩展的参数增量到CPT增强的基础模型上，解决了多语言扩展中参数冲突与能力保留的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据免融合技术在扩展LLM到新语言时面临关键权衡：缓解参数冲突以保留原始能力会稀释新语言习得，反之亦然。需要一种能同时保持原有能力并有效获取新语言能力的方法。

Method: 1) 将稠密模型升级为MoE架构，为不同语言分配不同专家；2) 通过移植MoE扩展的参数增量(Δ_post)到CPT增强的基础模型，绕过复杂的对齐阶段。

Result: 该方法在保持原始能力的同时提升了扩展语言的性能，优于具有相似FLOPs或参数数量的基线方法，且适用于不同模型和后训练增量。

Conclusion: METHOD方法通过MoE架构有效解决了多语言扩展中的参数冲突问题，实现了新语言获取与原有能力保留的双赢，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Expanding Large Language Models~(LLMs) to new languages is a costly endeavor, demanding extensive Continued Pre-Training~(CPT) and data-intensive alignment. While recent data-free merging techniques attempt to bypass alignment by fusing a multilingual CPT-enhanced model with its instruct counterpart, they are plagued by a critical trade-off: mitigating parameter conflicts to preserve original abilities inevitably dilutes new language acquisition, and vice-versa. To resolve this conflict, we introduce \method, which upcycles a dense model into a Mixture-of-Experts~(MoE) architecture, allocating different experts to different languages. Alignment ability is then transferred by grafting a MoE-expanded parameter delta~($Δ_{\text{post}}$) to the CPT-enhanced base model, bypassing the complex alignment phase. Experiments demonstrate \method's superiority even against baselines with similar FLOPs or number of parameters; it improves performance on expanded languages while effectively preserving original capabilities. We further show our approach is highly applicable across different models and Post-training deltas.

</details>


### [316] [How Loud Rumbles Hit Newsstands: A Data Analysis of Coverage and Spatial Bias in German News about Landslides Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18105)
*Brielen Madureira,Andreas Niekler,Marc Keuschnigg,Mariana Madruga de Brito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了25年间近6万篇德语新闻报道中关于全球滑坡事件的报道模式，发现存在对南欧和西欧的过度报道现象，揭示了媒体对国际灾害关注的不平等性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻是构建灾难数据库和进行媒体关注动态研究的重要数据源，但新闻数据集需要经过过滤、地理定位和验证。本文旨在探究德语报纸如何报道全球滑坡事件，并揭示媒体注意力分布的不平等现象。

Method: 分析近6万篇新闻文章（涵盖5.5千个新闻事件，时间跨度25年），并将新闻报道模式与国家滑坡易发性外部指标进行对比。

Result: 发现德语媒体对南欧和西欧的滑坡事件存在过度报道，与这些地区的实际滑坡易发性不匹配。

Conclusion: 揭示了媒体对国际灾害关注度的不平等性，为后续相关研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Landslides often hit newsstands due to their destructive and potentially fatal effects. News are a valuable source of information for creating or enriching disaster databases and for expediting media-based studies of the dynamics of media attention. To accomplish that, news datasets must be filtered, geolocated and validated. This paper focuses on how landslides around the world are reported in German newspapers. We analyse almost 60k news articles about 5.5k news events in a 25-year period, compare it with external measures of countries' susceptibility to landslides and provide insights, e.g.~the overreporting of Southern and Western Europe, to foment further studies on inequalities in media attention to international disasters.

</details>


### [317] [How Good LLMs Are at Answering Bangla Medical Visual Questions? Dataset and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18111)
*Rafid Ahmed,Intesar Tahmid,Mir Sazzat Hossain,Tasnimul Hossain Tomal,Md Fahim,Md Farhad Alam Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个孟加拉语医学视觉问答基准数据集BanglaMedVQA，评估了当前主流基础模型在该资源上的表现，发现即使是顶尖模型（如Gemini、GPT-4.1 mini）在低资源语言的细粒度医学推理上也表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语虽是全球使用最广泛的语言之一，但缺乏专门的MedVQA基准数据集，现有医学视觉问答研究主要集中于英语等资源丰富的语言，低资源语言的研究存在明显空白。

Method: 构建了包含临床验证的图像-问题-答案对的BanglaMedVQA数据集，并系统评估了当前多种基础模型（包括闭源和开源模型）在该基准上的表现。

Result: 孟加拉语MedVQA性能显著低于英语水平；Gemini和GPT-4.1 mini等顶尖模型在专业诊断问题上表现不佳；Gemma-3等开源模型在通用类别上偶尔更优，但在临床复杂问题上同样表现困难。

Conclusion: 当前模型在低资源语言的细粒度医学推理上存在严重局限，亟需更高质量的评估方法和针对性的模型优化。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have enabled general-purpose systems to demonstrate promising capabilities in complex reasoning tasks, including those in the medical domain. Medical Visual Question Answering (MedVQA) has particularly benefited from these developments. However, despite Bangla being one of the most widely spoken languages globally, there exists no established MedVQA benchmark for it. To address this gap, we introduce BanglaMedVQA, a dataset comprising clinically validated image-question-answer pairs, along with a comprehensive evaluation of current foundation models on this resource. Consistent with prior findings that report low performance of current models on English MedVQA benchmarks, our analysis reveals that Bangla performance is substantially lower, reflecting the challenges inherent to low-resource languages. Even top-performing models such as Gemini and GPT-4.1 mini fail to accurately answer specialized diagnostic questions, indicating severe limitations in fine-grained medical reasoning. Although certain open-source models, such as Gemma-3, occasionally outperform these models in general categories, they too struggle with clinically complex questions, underscoring the urgent need for top-notch evaluation method.

</details>


### [318] [iPOE: Interpretable Prompt Optimization via Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18113)
*Jiahui Li,Sean Papay,Roman Klinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为iPOE的可解释提示优化策略，通过自动生成或人工提供的注释决策解释来创建指南，并经过删减、添加、重排和合并等操作优化提示，使LLM的决策过程透明化。实验表明iPOE相比无指南和随机选择指南的提示分别提升高达31%和35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法被视为离散搜索问题，但结果不透明，无法解释为何特定提示变化带来性能提升，这与人类在注释任务中基于精心设计的指南进行标注的方式形成对比。

Method: 提出iPOE方法，利用注释决策的解释自动生成指南，通过删除、添加、重排和合并等操作对指南集进行优化，最终形成包含指南的提示，使LLM决策和优化过程透明化。

Result: 在四个数据集上，iPOE相比无指南的提示提升高达31%，相比随机选择指南的提示提升高达35%。同时，LLM生成的解释可以替代人工解释。

Conclusion: iPOE通过可解释的指南优化提示，提升了性能并增强了透明度，尤其适用于需要专业知识的领域，且非专业人士也能理解。

Abstract: Prompt optimization has often been framed as a discrete search problem to find high-performing and robust instructions for an LLM. However, the search result might not make it transparent why and where specific prompt changes lead to performance gains. This is in contrast to how humans are instructed for annotation tasks. Here, researchers carefully design annotation guidelines, leading to enhanced annotation consistency. Our paper aims at joining these two approaches and introduces iPOE, a novel interpretable prompt optimization strategy via explanations. We guide the prompt optimization process by automatically created guidelines from explanations of annotation decisions (either automatically generated or from humans). This set of guidelines is furthermore optimized by as series of operations, including removing, adding, shuffling, and merging. The resulting prompt includes guidelines that instruct the annotation, making the decision process of the LLM and the optimization transparent. It therefore supports also laypeople in the area of prompt optimization, particularly in challenging domains requiring expertise. In our experiments on four datasets, we find that iPOE can improves over prompts without guidelines and with random selected guidelines by up to $31\%$ and $35\%$, respectively. Moreover, LLM explanations can replace human explanations in the proposed method.

</details>


### [319] [FOL2NS: Generating Natural Sentences from First-Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18155)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了FOL2NS框架，用于将一阶逻辑公式自动转换为自然语言句子，通过结合规则驱动模块和微调语言模型生成合成数据，实验表明能生成良好模板和流畅语句，但在复杂结构下语义精确性和自然性仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式语言到自然语言的翻译是NLP的基础挑战，现有语料库很少能捕捉深度嵌套结构和不同量词深度的逻辑公式，缺乏高质量的合成训练数据。

Method: 提出FOL2NS（一阶逻辑到自然句子）框架，采用神经符号方法，结合规则驱动模块生成合成FOL公式，并通过微调语言模型将其转换为自然语言表达，支持不同量词深度和深层嵌套结构。

Result: FOL2NS能可靠生成结构良好的模板和流畅的语句，但随着结构复杂度增加，在精确语义表示和自然生成方面仍面临挑战。

Conclusion: FOL2NS在形式语言到自然语言的转换上取得了进展，但在处理高复杂度结构时仍需改进语义精度和生成自然度。

Abstract: Translating formal language into natural language is a foundational challenge in NLP, driving various downstream applications in semantic parsing, theorem validation, and question answering. In this study, we introduce First-Order Logic to Natural Sentence (FOL2NS), a neurosymbolic framework designed to generate synthetic FOL formulas and convert them into natural human expressions. It handles deeply nested structures with varying quantifier depths (QD), which are rarely captured by existing corpora. By combining rule-driven modules with fine-tuned language models, FOL2NS enhances the diversity and coverage of the generated samples. In our experiments, we systematically evaluate the framework's capabilities through both character-level analysis and overall performance metrics. Experimental results show that FOL2NS can reliably produce well-formed templates and fluent statements, but it faces challenges in achieving precise semantic representations and natural generation as structural complexity increases.

</details>


### [320] [Context Memorization for Efficient Long Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18226)
*Yasuyuki Okoshi,Hao Mark Chen,Guanxi Lu,Hongxiang Fan,Masato Motomura,Daichi Fujiki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的注意力状态记忆方法，通过将前缀的注意力状态外部化为轻量级查找表，解决了长前缀推理中注意力衰减和计算开销大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长前缀增强推理方法存在两个结构性问题：一是前缀的影响随生成过程逐渐衰减，二是前缀的注意力计算量与其长度呈线性增长。现有方法要么仍保留前缀注意力计算，要么需要昂贵的梯度训练且不适合前缀更新。

Method: 提出注意力状态记忆（attention-state memory），一种无需训练的方法，将前缀外部化为预计算的注意力状态轻量级查找表（lookup-based memory），在推理时直接查表而非重新计算。

Result: 在ManyICLBench上，使用LLaMA-3.1-8B，在1K-8K记忆预算下准确率超过上下文学习，8K时注意力延迟降低1.36倍；在NBA基准上，仅用20%记忆量就超越了全注意力RAG性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持性能的同时显著降低了推理开销，且无需训练即可灵活更新前缀，为长前缀推理提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) applications increasingly rely on long conditioning prefixes to control model behavior at inference time. While prefix-augmented inference is effective, it incurs two structural limitations: i) the prefix's influence fades as generation proceeds, and ii) attention computation over the prefix scales linearly with its length. Existing approaches either keep the prefix in attention while compressing it, or internalize it into model parameters through gradient-based training. The former still attends to the prefix at inference, while the latter is training-intensive and ill-suited to prefix updates. To address these issues, we propose attention-state memory, a training-free approach that externalizes the prefix into a lightweight, lookup-based memory of precomputed attention states between prefix and query tokens. On ManyICLBench with LLaMA-3.1-8B, our method improves accuracy over in-context learning at 1K-8K memory budgets while reducing attention latency by 1.36x at 8K, and surpasses full-attention RAG performance on NBA benchmark using only 20% of its memory footprint.

</details>


### [321] [SomaliWeb v1: A Quality-Filtered Somali Web Corpus with a Matched Tokenizer and a Public Language-Identification Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18232)
*Khalid Yusuf Dahir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个专为索马里语设计的预训练语料库SomaliWeb v1（约3.03亿token），配套BPE-16K分词器及语言识别基准，揭示了现有多语言语料库中索马里语数据存在大量重复、乱码等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 索马里语拥有约2500万使用者，但缺乏公开的、专门构建的预训练语料库、分词器及语言识别基准，现有数据分散在多语言语料库中且质量未经评估。

Method: 从HPLT v2、CC100和索马里语维基百科三个上游来源，通过六阶段可复现流水线构建质量过滤后的语料库，并开发BPE-16K分词器。

Result: SomaliWeb v1包含819,322篇文档（约3.03亿token）。发现HPLT v2中17.3%的文档为完全重复、56.1%存在可修复的乱码、10.7%为近似重复。BPE-16K分词器在FLORES-200测试集上比GPT-4的cl100k_base少生成40.2%的token。

Conclusion: 发布高质量的索马里语语料库、分词器及语言识别基准，为低资源语言NLP研究提供基础资源，并揭示现有数据的质量问题。

Abstract: Somali is a Cushitic language of the Horn of Africa with ~25 million speakers, yet no documented dedicated Somali pretraining corpus with a companion tokenizer and language-identification benchmark has been publicly released. Existing Somali text appears either inside multilingual distributions (HPLT v2, CC100, MADLAD-400, OSCAR, mC4) or in small, undocumented Somali-only uploads on Hugging Face. We introduce SomaliWeb v1, a quality-filtered Somali corpus of 819,322 documents (~303M tokens) built from three upstream sources (HPLT v2, CC100, Somali Wikipedia) through a six-stage reproducible pipeline. We release (i) the corpus, (ii) a matched BPE-16K tokenizer, and (iii) the first public side-by-side Somali benchmark of three production language identifiers. Our measurements reveal concrete quality defects in existing distributions: HPLT v2's "cleaned" Somali release retains 17.3% byte-exact duplicates, 56.1% of its documents contain fixable mojibake, and 10.7% of its byte-unique documents are near-duplicates at Jaccard tau=0.80. Our BPE-16K tokenizer emits 40.2% fewer tokens than GPT-4's cl100k_base on FLORES-200 Somali devtest as a tokenizer-level measurement; downstream language-model perplexity comparisons are deferred to a follow-up release.

</details>


### [322] [Multilingual jailbreaking of LLMs using low-resource languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18239)
*Dylan Marx,Marcel Dunaiski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用低资源非洲语言（如南非荷兰语、斯瓦希里语等）进行多轮对话来绕过LLM安全机制的方法，发现翻译质量是决定越狱成功的关键因素，多轮对话越狱率较高（41.8%-83.6%），且人类红队测试比自动化方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型即使有安全护栏，仍然容易受到越狱攻击。现有研究多关注高资源语言，而对低资源语言（尤其是非洲语言）中的安全漏洞探索不足，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从现有数据集翻译提示词，使用非洲低资源语言（南非荷兰语、斯瓦希里语、科萨语、祖鲁语）对ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek、Gemini和Grok进行自动化测试和母语者人工红队测试。

Result: 单轮翻译攻击无效；多轮对话中英语有害响应率为52.7%-83.6%，南非荷兰语为60.0%-78.2%，斯瓦希里语为41.8%-70.9%。人工红队测试比自动化方法越狱率更高，平均越狱率从59.8%提升至75.8%。

Conclusion: LLM在多语言环境下仍存在安全漏洞，翻译质量是决定低资源语言越狱成功的关键因素，越狱成功并非仅仅因为使用低资源语言，而是由于翻译质量差导致安全机制失效。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attempts that circumvent safety guardrails. We investigate whether multi-turn conversations using low-resource African languages (Afrikaans, Kiswahili, isiXhosa, and isiZulu) can bypass safety mechanisms across commercial LLMs. We translated prompts from existing datasets and evaluated ChatGPT, Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, and Grok through automated testing and human red-teaming with native speakers. Single-turn translation attacks proved ineffective, while multi-turn conversations achieved English harmful response rates from 52.7% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 83.6% (GPT-4o-mini), Afrikaans from 60.0% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 78.2% (GPT-4o-mini), and Kiswahili from 41.8% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 70.9% (DeepSeek). Human red-teaming increased jailbreak rates compared to automated methods. Over all evaluated languages, the average jailbreak rate increased from 59.8% to 75.8%, with improvements of +20.0% (Afrikaans), +12.7% (isiZulu), +12.3% (isiXhosa), and +1% (Kiswahili), demonstrating that poor translation quality limits jailbreak success. These findings suggest that vulnerabilities in LLMs persist in multilingual contexts and that translation quality is the critical factor determining jailbreak success in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [323] [Knowledge-to-Verification: Exploring RLVR for LLMs in Knowledge-Intensive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18261)
*Zhonghang Yuan,Zhefan Wang,Fang Hu,Zihong Chen,Jinzhe Li,Gang Li,Jie Ying,Huanjun Kong,Songyang Zhang,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: K2V框架通过自动化可验证数据合成和推理过程验证，将RLVR扩展到知识密集型领域，在不显著降低通用能力的前提下提升了大模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR主要应用于数学和编程领域，在知识密集型领域缺乏高质量可验证数据；同时RLVR仅关注最终答案的正确性，存在推理过程有缺陷和奖励信号稀疏的问题。

Method: 提出Knowledge-to-Verification (K2V)框架，通过自动化可验证数据合成将RLVR扩展到知识密集型领域，并实现对LLM推理过程的验证。

Result: 实验表明K2V能增强LLM在知识密集型领域的推理能力，且不会显著损害模型的一般能力。

Conclusion: 将自动化数据合成与推理验证相结合是提升模型在更广泛领域能力的有效方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated promising potential to enhance the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in domains such as mathematics and coding. However, its applications on knowledge-intensive domains have not been effectively explored due to the scarcity of high-quality verifiable data. Furthermore, current RLVR focuses solely on the correctness of final answers, leading to the limitations of flawed reasoning and sparse reward signals. In this work, we propose Knowledge-to-Verification (K2V), a framework that extends RLVR to knowledge-intensive domains through automated verifiable data synthesis, while enabling verification of the LLM's reasoning process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that K2V enhances the reasoning of LLM in knowledge-intensive domains without significantly compromising the model's general capabilities. This study also suggests that integrating automated data synthesis with reasoning verification is a promising direction to enhance model capabilities in these broader domains. Code is available at https://github.com/SeedScientist/K2V.

</details>


### [324] [Infini-News: Efficiently Queryable Access to 1.3 Billion Processed Common Crawl News Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18337)
*Ruggero Marino Lazzaroni,Jana Lasser,Kirill Solovev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Infini-News，一个覆盖CC-News档案（2016年至今、超过13.5亿篇文章）的检索工具包与索引，包含文本清洗、语言检测、地理归因及Infini-gram索引，极大降低了大规模新闻语料的获取与使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模新闻语料库在获取上存在商业档案成本高昂、开放替代品（如CC-News）需海量存储与计算资源的双重障碍，亟需一种低成本、高效率的检索方案。

Method: 1) 提取、清洗并解析了超过13.5亿篇文章的结构化元数据；2) 使用三种前沿语言分类器（GlotLID、lingua、CommonLingua）进行语言检测，并通过多源地理归因为83.4%的文章标注了222个国家/地区的来源国；3) 构建了Infini-gram索引（后缀数组结构），支持亚秒级任意文本模式搜索。

Result: 成功构建了覆盖全量CC-News档案的检索工具包与索引，实现了亚秒级文本模式搜索，并为83.4%的文章提供了地理归因。

Conclusion: Infini-News有效降低了纵向、跨国媒体研究的门槛，为计算社会科学和NLP领域的大规模新闻分析提供了便捷的基础设施。

Abstract: Large-scale news corpora support a wide range of research in Computational Social Science and NLP, yet access remains constrained: commercial archives impose prohibitive costs and licensing restrictions, while open alternatives like Common Crawl's CC-News require terabyte-scale storage and computationally intensive processing. We present Infini-News, a retrieval toolkit and index for the entire CC-News archive from August 2016 to the latest available snapshot. Our contributions are threefold. First, we extract, clean the text, and parse the structured metadata of over 1.35B articles. Second, we enrich the corpus with language detection using three frontier language classifiers (GlotLID, lingua, and CommonLingua), and with multi-source geographic attribution that resolves a country of origin for 83.4% of articles across 222 countries. Third, we construct Infini-gram indexes: suffix-array structures that let researchers search the full archive for arbitrary text patterns in sub-second time. Together, these resources lower the barrier to longitudinal, cross-national media research.

</details>


### [325] [Presupposition and Reasoning in Conditionals: A Theory-Based Study of Humans and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18352)
*Tara Azin,Yongan Yu,Raj Singh,Olessia Jouravlev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过平行行为实验，比较了人类和大型语言模型在条件句中预设投射的判断，发现LLMs的表现更多依赖表面模式匹配而非真正的语用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件句中的预设投射是意义与语用理论的核心问题，但在大语言模型中尚未得到系统评估，作者希望通过填补这一空白来检验LLMs的语用能力。

Method: 基于规范化的条件句数据集，收集120名人类参与者和4个LLMs在匹配语境下的可能性评分，并采用LLM-as-a-Judge框架结合语言学启发的检查表评估模型推理。

Result: 人类整合了概率和语用线索进行判断；LLMs在人类模式对齐上存在差异；最匹配人类评分的模型往往缺乏连贯的语用推理，而推理能力更强的模型反而产生更不类人的判断。

Conclusion: LLMs在此类任务上的表现可能源于表面模式匹配而非语用能力，强调了基于语言学理论的基准测试对于比较人类与模型的重要性。

Abstract: Presupposition projection in conditionals is central to theories of meaning and pragmatics, yet it remains largely unevaluated in large language models. We address this gap through a parallel behavioral study comparing human judgments and LLM predictions on a normed dataset of conditional sentences that controls the relation between the antecedent and the projected presupposition. We collect likelihood ratings from 120 participants and four LLMs under matched contextual conditions. Results show that humans integrate probabilistic and pragmatic cues in their judgment, whereas LLMs show variable alignment with human patterns. Using a linguistically motivated checklist within an LLM-as-a-Judge framework, we further evaluate model reasoning. We observe models that best match human ratings often lack coherent pragmatic reasoning, while models with stronger reasoning produce less human-like judgments. These findings suggest that LLMs' performance on such tasks may result from surface pattern matching rather than pragmatic competence. Our findings highlight the importance of benchmarks grounded in linguistic theory for comparing humans and models.

</details>


### [326] [SkillsVote: Lifecycle Governance of Agent Skills from Collection, Recommendation to Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18401)
*Hongyi Liu,Haoyan Yang,Tao Jiang,Bo Tang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SkillsVote框架，用于管理LLM智能体的技能生命周期（收集→推荐→演化），通过结构化的技能库和经验门控更新机制，在不更新模型的情况下提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体产生的轨迹包含可复用经验，但原始轨迹噪声大且难以管理。开放的技能生态存在冗余、质量不均、环境敏感等问题，不加区分的更新会污染未来上下文。

Method: SkillsVote框架包含：(1) 对百万级开源语料进行环境要求、质量和可验证性分析；(2) 生成可验证技能的任务；(3) 执行前进行结构化技能库的智能检索；(4) 执行后将轨迹分解为技能相关子任务，归因于技能使用、智能体探索、环境和结果信号；(5) 仅允许成功的可重用发现通过证据门控更新。

Result: 离线演化在Terminal-Bench 2.0上提升GPT-5.2最多7.9个百分点；在线演化在SWE-Bench Pro上提升最多2.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过控制暴露、归因和保存，受治理的外部技能库可以在不更新模型的情况下提升冻结模型性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents leave traces that could become reusable experience, but raw trajectories are noisy and hard to govern. We treat Agent Skills as an experience schema that couples executable scripts, with non-executable guidance on procedures. Yet open skill ecosystems contain redundant, uneven, environment-sensitive artifacts, and indiscriminate updates can pollute future context. We present SkillsVote, a lifecycle-governance framework for Agent Skills from collection and recommendation to evolution. SkillsVote profiles a million-scale open-source corpus for environment requirements, quality, and verifiability, then synthesizes tasks for verifiable skills. Before execution, SkillsVote performs agentic library search over structured skill library to expose instructional skill context. After execution, it decomposes trajectories into skill-linked subtasks, attributes outcomes to skill use, agent exploration, environment, and result signals, and admits only successful reusable discoveries to evidence-gated updates. In our evaluation, offline evolution improves GPT-5.2 on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by up to 7.9 pp, while online evolution improves SWE-Bench Pro by up to 2.6 pp. Overall, governed external skill libraries can improve frozen agents without model updates when systems control exposure, credit, and preservation.

</details>


### [327] [From BERT to T5: A Study of Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18462)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本报告对BERT（编码器+分类头）和T5（序列到序列+少样本提示）两种预训练模型在命名实体识别任务上进行了微调与对比，包括7类/3类标签方案、加权交叉熵、验证策略及消融实验，最终通过性能指标比较了两者在序列标注任务中的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NER是NLP应用中的关键预处理步骤，当前需要系统比较不同架构（编码器模型与序列到序列模型）在该任务上的表现，以指导实际应用中的模型选择。

Method: 使用BERT（编码器模型+简单分类头）和T5（序列到序列模型+少样本提示）进行微调；采用原始7类标签和简化3类标签方案；BERT使用加权交叉熵损失，T5采用两种验证策略；并进行了超参数消融实验。

Result: 通过多项性能指标对比了两架构表现，分析了BERT常见错误类型及两种模型各自的性能特点。

Conclusion: 该研究为序列标注任务中编码器模型与序列到序列模型的比较提供了依据，为后续实际应用场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: Named entity recognition (NER) has been one of the essential preliminary steps in modern NLP applications. This report focuses on implementing the NER task on finetuning two pretrained models: (i) an encoder-only model (BERT) with a simple classification head, and (ii) a sequence-to-sequence model (T5) with few-shot prompts. Under the original 7-class tag and 3-class simplified tag schemes, BERT is applied a weighted cross-entropy for training loss, and T5 is fine-tuned with two validation strategies. It also conducted an ablation study with different hyperparameters. Moreover, the related analysis provides valuable insights into common errors in BERT and the two models' performance. Based on a bunch of performance metrics, this report aims to compare the above two architectures and explore their abilities in the sequence labelling task, laying the groundwork for further practical use cases.

</details>


### [328] [Vector RAG vs LLM-Compiled Wiki: A Preregistered Comparison on a Small Multi-Domain Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18490)
*Theodore O. Cochran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 比较了单轮向量RAG和LLM编译的Markdown Wiki两种方法，帮助LLM回答小型研究语料库中的问题。Wiki在跨论文连接发现上表现更好，但RAG在单事实查找上更优。两者在不同维度各有优劣，没有一种架构在所有方面都最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究两种不同架构（向量RAG vs. LLM编译的Markdown Wiki）在帮助LLM回答研究语料问题时的效果差异，特别是跨论文综合能力、引用准确性和成本效益方面。

Method: 预注册比较实验：两种系统分别回答针对24篇论文的13个问题，使用相同的答案生成模型，答案由盲法LLM评判员评分。还包括探索性分析：声明级别引用检查和基于分解的RAG变体。

Result: Wiki在跨论文连接方面得分更高，但组织优势在调整后不显著；RAG在单事实查找上达到预注册检验标准；Wiki查询端token成本远高于RAG，无法通过更便宜查询回收前期构建成本；声明级引用检查显示Wiki更优；分解RAG变体恢复了大部分Wiki的跨论文综合优势，但未恢复引用支持优势。

Conclusion: 有根据的研究综合不是单一能力，系统在证据组织、引用支持和运行成本三个维度上表现不同，本研究中没有一种架构在所有方面都最佳。

Abstract: We preregistered a comparison of two ways to help an LLM answer questions over a small research corpus: a single-round Vector RAG system and an LLM-compiled markdown wiki. Both systems answered the same 13 questions over 24 papers using the same answer-generating model, and their answers were scored by blinded LLM judges.
  The wiki scored much better at connecting findings across papers, but its advantage in answer organization was not strong after judge adjustment. RAG met the preregistered test for single-fact lookup questions. The clean query-side cost result went against the expected wiki advantage: under the tested setup, the wiki used far more query tokens than RAG, so it could not recover any upfront build cost through cheaper queries.
  Two exploratory analyses changed how we interpret the result. First, claim-level citation checking favored the wiki: its cited pages more often supported the exact claims being made, even though RAG scored better on the overall groundedness rubric. Second, a decomposition-based RAG variant recovered most of the wiki's advantage on cross-paper synthesis at lower LLM-token cost, but it did not recover the wiki advantage in claim-by-claim citation support.
  The main conclusion is that grounded research synthesis is not a single capability. Systems can differ in how well they organize evidence, how well their citations support each claim, and how much they cost to run. In this study, no architecture was best on all three.

</details>


### [329] [Implicit Hierarchical GRPO: Decoupling Tool Invocation from Execution for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18500)
*Li Wang,Xiaohan Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Zipeng Zhang,Jinyang Wu,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出延迟执行策略解耦工具调用与执行过程，通过隐式分层策略算法IH-GRPO提升LLM推理性能，在多个数学推理基准上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将工具调用与立即执行紧密耦合，这会打断LLM的推理连贯性并限制其表达能力，最终降低推理性能。

Method: 首次提出并形式化解耦工具调用与执行的问题，引入延迟执行与显式控制；提出分层控制框架，推导出替代损失函数使隐式分层策略学习等价于显式分层策略的行为，进而提出IH-GRPO算法。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上，跨六个域外数学推理基准较最强基线分别提升1.87%、2.16%、2.53%，并在其他领域也取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 延迟工具执行策略能有效提升LLM工具增强推理的性能与连贯性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly leveraged tool invocation to enhance their reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches typically tightly couple tool invocation with immediate execution. Such immediate tool interaction may disrupt the reasoning coherence of LLMs and constrain their expressivity, ultimately degrading reasoning performance. To this end, for the first time, we propose and formalize the problem of decoupling tool invocation from execution during reasoning, and introduce delayed execution with explicit control to enhance tool-integrated reasoning (TIR). Furthermore, we propose a hierarchical control framework and theoretically derive a surrogate loss that enables an implicitly hierarchical policy to learn behavior equivalent to that of an explicit hierarchical policy, leading to the proposed IH-GRPO algorithm. Extensive experiments on IH-GRPO achieve absolute improvements of 1.87\%, 2.16\%, and 2.53\% on Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B across six out-of-domain mathematical reasoning benchmarks over the strongest baseline method, while also yielding consistent performance gains in other domains. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/IH-GRPO-01.

</details>


### [330] [Ancient Greek to Modern Greek Machine Translation: A Novel Benchmark and Fine-Tuning Experiments on LLMs and NMT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18504)
*Spyridon Mavromatis,Sokratis Sofianopoulos,Prokopis Prokopidis,Maria Giagkou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对古希腊语到现代希腊语的低资源机器翻译任务，构建了包含13.2万句对的AG-MG平行语料库，提出结合VecAlign、微调LaBSE嵌入和LLM纠错的对齐流水线，并首次系统评估了NMT模型和希腊语LLM的翻译性能，最高BLEU达13.16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语到现代希腊语的机器翻译缺少大规模高质量平行数据，现有资源严重不足，亟需构建专用语料库并建立基准评测。

Method: (1) 通过网页抓取获取片段级数据，经多阶段句级对齐与精炼构建AG-MG平行语料库；(2) 使用微调后的LaBSE嵌入进行VecAlign对齐，再用Gemini 2.5 Flash进行LLM纠错；(3) 评估NLLB、M2M100和Llama-Krikri-8B三种模型，对比全参数微调与QLoRA适配策略。

Result: 全参数微调的Llama-Krikri-8B取得最高BLEU 13.16；QLoRA适配的M2M100-1.2B增益最大且极具竞争力；微调相比基线最高提升+10.3 BLEU。

Conclusion: 该研究为古希腊语-现代希腊语MT提供了首个大规模平行语料库和系统基准，显著提升了翻译性能，是对希腊语NLP的重要贡献。

Abstract: Machine Translation (MT) for Ancient Greek (AG) to Modern Greek (MG) is a low-resource task, constrained by the lack of large-scale, high-quality parallel data. We address this gap by introducing the AG-MG Parallel Corpus, a new resource containing 132,481 sentence-aligned pairs derived from literary, historical, and biblical texts. We present a novel corpus creation pipeline that combines web-scraped, excerpt-level data with a multi-stage sentence-level alignment, and refinement process. Our method uses VecAlign with LaBSE embeddings, which we first fine-tune on a manually-aligned AG-MG subset, followed by an LLM-based error/misalignment correction phase using Gemini 2.5 Flash to ensure high alignment quality. Furthermore, we provide the first comprehensive benchmark of modern MT models on this task, evaluating three fine-tuning strategies across NMT models (NLLB, M2M100) and a Greek LLM (Llama-Krikri-8B). Our experiments show that fine-tuning yields significant improvements over base models, increasing performance by up to +10.3 BLEU points. Specifically, full-parameter fine-tuning of Llama-Krikri-8B achieves the highest overall performance with a BLEU score of 13.16, while the QLoRA-adapted M2M100-1.2B model demonstrates the largest relative gains and highly competitive results. Our dataset and models represent a significant contribution to Greek NLP.

</details>


### [331] [Easier to Judge than to Find: Predicting In-Context Learning Success for Demonstration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18512)
*Haochun Wang,Chaofen Yang,Jiatong Liu,Jingbo Wang,Zewen Qiang,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DiSP框架，通过"先采样再评判"策略，将查询按难度分层，用轻量级路由器预测难度并训练不同级别的评判器，在保持高准确率的同时实现高达23倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL对示例选择高度敏感，但现有选择方法成本极高，因为可能的示例组合空间巨大。核心洞察是"判断比搜索更容易"——预测某个查询-示例对是否有效比搜索最优示例更高效、更通用。

Method: DiSP框架包含三阶段：1) 随机采样示例进行试验，估算每个训练查询的成功率；2) 训练轻量级路由器从查询本身预测难度；3) 为不同难度级别训练专门的评判器。推理时采用"接受即停止"策略，在显式预算下进行评判，当找不到合适上下文时发出诊断风险标签。

Result: 在5个分类数据集上使用Llama 3-8B和Qwen 2.5-7B，DiSP取得了最佳平均准确率，比强学习基线提升高达3.4%，同时端到端加速高达23倍。

Conclusion: DiSP证明了"判断比搜索更容易"这一洞察的有效性，通过难度分层和轻量级评判器，在ICL中实现了准确率与效率的双赢。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is highly sensitive to which demonstrations appear in the prompt, but selecting them is expensive because the space of possible demonstration contexts and combinations is enormous. We argue that demonstration selection is \emph{easier to judge than to find}: predicting whether a specific query--context pair $(q,D)$ will succeed is cheaper and more general than searching for an optimal $D^\star$. Based on this insight, we propose DiSP, a sample-and-judge framework that stratifies queries by difficulty. DiSP runs random demonstration trials to estimate success rate of each training query, trains a lightweight router to predict difficulty from the query, and trains level-specific judges for sampled demonstrations. At inference, DiSP performs stop-on-acceptance judging under an explicit budget, emitting diagnostic risk tags when no suitable context is found. Across five classification datasets with Llama~3--8B and Qwen~2.5--7B, DiSP achieves the best average accuracy, improving over strong learned selection baselines by up to 3.4\%, while achieving up to $23\times$ end-to-end wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [332] [STT-Arena: A More Realistic Environment for Tool-Using with Spatio-Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18548)
*Tingfeng Hui,Hao Xu,Pengyu Zhu,Hongsheng Xin,Kun Zhan,Sen Su,Chunxiao Liu,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STT-Arena基准，评估LLM在空间-时间动态环境中重新规划的能力。前沿模型准确率不足40%。基于错误分析提出的STT-Agent-4B模型性能超越前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态基准主要检测LLM能否及时感知时间变化，但缺乏对模型在时空动态环境下适应性重规划能力的评估。

Method: 构建STT-Arena基准（227个交互任务，涵盖9种时空冲突类型和4个可解性层级），配备可注入的时空触发器来突然破坏已有规划。通过错误分析识别三种错误模式，提出迭代轨迹精炼技术并结合在线强化学习训练STT-Agent-4B。

Result: 最先进的闭源模型（如Claude-4.6-Opus）在STT-Arena上准确率不到40%。STT-Agent-4B（仅4B参数）超越了这些前沿LLM。

Conclusion: 时空动态推理对当前LLM极具挑战性；通过分析失败模式并针对性优化，小模型也能在特定场景下超越大模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in real-world agentic applications must be capable of replanning and adapting when mid-task disruptions invalidate their prior decisions. Existing dynamic benchmarks primarily measure whether LLMs can detect temporal changes in a timely manner, leaving the complementary challenge of adaptive replanning under spatio-temporal dynamics largely unexplored. We introduce STT-Arena (Spatio-Temporal Tool-Use Arena), a benchmark of 227 high-quality interactive tasks spanning nine spatio-temporal conflict types and four solvability levels. Each task is grounded in a realistic, executable environment equipped with injected spatio-temporal triggers that can abruptly invalidate an ongoing plan, forcing the model to detect the state shift and construct a revised execution strategy. Extensive evaluation of frontier LLMs reveals that even the SOTA proprietary models, including Claude-4.6-Opus, achieves less than 40\% overall accuracies, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of spatio-temporal dynamic reasoning. Systematic analysis of failure trajectories uncovers three recurring error modes of existing models: Stale-State Execution, Misdiagnosis of Dynamic Triggers, and Missing Post-Adaptation Verification. Guided by these findings, we propose an iterative trajectory refinement technique that eliminates these failure patterns from training data, and combine it with online RL to produce STT-Agent-4B which outperforms frontier LLMs on STT-Arena.

</details>


### [333] [Monitoring the Internal Monologue: Probe Trajectories Reveal Reasoning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18549)
*Maciej Chrabąszcz,Aleksander Szymczyk,Marcin Sendera,Tomasz Trzciński,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过分析大推理模型的隐藏表示和思维链（CoT）轨迹，提出探针轨迹框架，利用信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态）来预测模型未来行为，最高可达95% AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽为安全监控提供新机会，但其与最终输出并非总是一致，降低了作为监控工具的可靠性，因此需要探索更可靠的LRM行为监控方法。

Method: 在每个生成token上设置探针，构建探针轨迹（概念概率在推理过程中的连续演化）；提取信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态）；使用模板训练数据替代动态生成数据；对比不同池化操作（平均池化、最后token、最大池化）。

Result: 基于完整探针轨迹的预测优于单次静态预测；最大池化达95% AUROC，而平均池化和最后token方法近随机；模板数据与动态生成数据效果接近。

Conclusion: 探针轨迹可作为监控LRM行为的互补框架，其轨迹特征编码了任务特定的动态信息，能够有效提高未来行为状态的可区分性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) introduce new opportunities for safety monitoring through their Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, CoT is not always faithful to the model's final output, undermining its reliability as a monitoring tool. To address this, we investigate the hidden representations of LRMs to determine whether future behavior can be predicted from prompt and CoT representations. By evaluating a probe at each generated token, we construct a probe trajectory, the continuous evolution of a concept's probability across the reasoning process. We find that future model behavior is more distinguishable when examined over the full trajectory than from a single static prediction. To characterize these temporal dynamics, we extract signal-processing features that capture volatility, trend, and steady-state behavior, significantly improving the separation of future model states. We also present two methodological insights. First, template-based training data achieves near-parity with dynamically generated model responses, eliminating the need for a costly initial inference and labeling.
